Sub Makro1()
	Set MTable=Doc.CreateTableObject("MAKROTABLE=STACK","IO")
	MTable.Addfield2 "RAPORPB","CHAR",4,0,"L","","*PUNIT"
	' MTable.Addfield2 "E_TARIH","DATE",10,0,"L","",""
	
	Set MTableT=Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("MAKROTABLET=STACK","IO")
	MTableT.Addfield2 "OP_NO","CHAR",10,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "KOD","CHAR",24,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('STOK00')",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "AD","CHAR",50,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW(""STOK00"",""KOD=KOD"",""AD"",""BRW_STOK00_AD"")",""
	
	MTableT.SetMacroModuleTableName "MAKROTABLET"
	MTableT.Setd7FieldEvent "KOD","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "KOD_OV"
	
	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing
	Doc.LoadSubForm_Dyn "MAKROFORMU1",SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME,Par
	
	MTable.RAPORPB = "EURO"
	MTableT.SetRowAID 1,1,1
End Sub
Sub MiktarSureHesapla()
	if OpNoGirilmemis Then Exit Sub
	Call Hesapla("MIKTAR_SURE")
End Sub
Sub MaliyetHesapla()
	Set gg = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")
	if OpNoGirilmemis Then Exit Sub
	Call Hesapla("MALIYET")
End Sub
Sub Hesapla(HesapOp)
	Set RepTable = GetRaporTable()
	Set MTableE = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET")
	
	Call DefaultBasliklariEkle(RepTable)

	RaporPB = MTableE.RAPORPB
	For i = 1 to MTableT.GetRowCount()
		if trim(MTableT.KOD(i)) <> "" Then
			Call AtolyeKayitlariniOku(MTableT.KOD(i), MTableT.OP_NO(i), RepTable, RaporPB)
			if HesapOp = "MALIYET" Then Call ModelMasraflariniOku(MTableT.KOD(i), MTableT.OP_NO(i), RepTable, RaporPB)
			Call HammaddeleriOku(MTableT.KOD(i), MTableT.OP_NO(i), RepTable, RaporPB)
			if HesapOp = "MALIYET" Then Call FasonMasraflariniOku(MTableT.KOD(i), MTableT.OP_NO(i), RepTable, RaporPB)
			if HesapOp = "MALIYET" Then Call MuhasebeMasraflariOku(MTableT.KOD(i), MTableT.OP_NO(i), RepTable, RaporPB)
		End if
	Next

	Set Excel = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
	Set Wb1 = Excel.Workbooks.Add
	
	RepTable.Sort("BOMREC_SIRANO+OP_NO+RAPOR_GRUBU+RAPOR_ALT_GRUBU")
	Call DetaylariYazdir(Excel, Wb1, RepTable)

	RepTable.GroupBy("BOMREC_SIRANO+OP_NO+RAPOR_GRUBU+RAPOR_ALT_GRUBU")
	Call RaporYazdir(Excel, Wb1, RepTable, HesapOp)
	'Call SendTableToExcel(RepTable)
End Sub
Function GetRaporTable()
	Doc.CreateTableObject "xxx___REP=STACK", "TMPCOPY"
	Set GetRaporTable = Doc.GetTableObject("xxx___REP")
	GetRaporTable.AddField2 "EVRAKNO","CHAR",10,0,"R","",""
	GetRaporTable.AddField2 "OP_NO","CHAR",10,0,"L","",""
	GetRaporTable.AddField2 "BOMREC_SIRANO","CHAR",10,0,"L","",""
	GetRaporTable.AddField2 "BOMREC_ALT_SIRANO","CHAR",10,0,"L","",""
	GetRaporTable.AddField2 "BOMREC_CODE","CHAR",24,0,"L","",""
	GetRaporTable.AddField2 "BOMREC_INPUTTYPE","CHAR",1,0,"L","",""
	GetRaporTable.AddField2 "BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE","CHAR",24,0,"L","",""
	GetRaporTable.AddField2 "BOMREC_KAYNAK0","DOUBLE",24,6,"R","",""
	GetRaporTable.AddField2 "BOMREC_KAYNAK1","DOUBLE",24,6,"R","",""
	GetRaporTable.AddField2 "SF_UNIT","CHAR",6,0,"L","",""
	GetRaporTable.AddField2 "RAPOR_GRUBU","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	GetRaporTable.AddField2 "RAPOR_ALT_GRUBU","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	GetRaporTable.AddField2 "OR_FIYAT","DOUBLE",20,4,"R","",""
	GetRaporTable.AddField2 "PRICEUNIT","CHAR",4,0,"L","",""
	GetRaporTable.AddField2 "OR_TUTAR","DOUBLE",20,2,"R","",""
	GetRaporTable.AddField2 "RPB_TUTAR","DOUBLE",20,2,"R","",""
	GetRaporTable.AddField2 "RPB_OPTUTAR","DOUBLE",20,2,"R","",""
	GetRaporTable.AddField2 "SURE_HARIC","CHAR",1,0,"L","",""
End Function
Sub AtolyeKayitlariniOku(StokKodu, OpNo, RepTable, RaporPB)

	' Set g = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")
	Set S20T = Doc.Select1G("dw4r5gggg", "SFDC20T", "MAMULKOD>='" & trim(StokKodu) & "'&MAMULKOD<='" & trim(StokKodu) & "zzzzz'", "","EVRAKTYPE+EVRAKNO+JOBNO+RECTARIH1+RECTIME1",-1)
	Call PresAltiCalismasiIcinTezgahSureleriniHesapla(S20T)

	For i = 1 to S20T.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Atölye Kayýtlarý Okunuyor : " & i & " / " & S20T.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = RepTable.AddRow()
		GK4 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("IMLT00", "GK_4", S20T.KOD(i), "")
		GK5 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("IMLT00", "GK_5", S20T.KOD(i), "")
		if trim(S20T.KOD(i)) = "MUH" Then
			RepTable.BOMREC_SIRANO(satirNo) = "01"
			RepTable.RAPOR_GRUBU(satirNo) = "CAD/CAM/PROJE TAKIP"
			RepTable.RAPOR_ALT_GRUBU(satirNo) = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("IMLT01", "AD", S20T.OPERASYON(i), "")
			MesaiVar = False
		Elseif trim(S20T.OPERASYON(i)) = "MÜÞTES" Then
			RepTable.BOMREC_SIRANO(satirNo) = "09"
			RepTable.RAPOR_GRUBU(satirNo) = "MÜÞTERÝ PRESÝNDE TESLÝM"
			RepTable.RAPOR_ALT_GRUBU(satirNo) = "ÝÞÇÝLÝK"
			MesaiVar = False
		Elseif trim(GK4) = "TAL" Then
			RepTable.BOMREC_SIRANO(satirNo) = "05"
			RepTable.BOMREC_ALT_SIRANO(satirNo) = trim(Doc.GetGecoValue("I00GK5", "AD_L2", GK5, -1))
			RepTable.RAPOR_GRUBU(satirNo) = "TALAÞLI ÝMALAT"
			RepTable.RAPOR_ALT_GRUBU(satirNo) = trim(Doc.GetGecoValue("I00GK5", "AD", GK5, -1))

			MesaiVar = True
			TalasliImalat = True
		Elseif trim(GK4) = "KALIP DENE" Then
			RepTable.BOMREC_SIRANO(satirNo) = "06"
			RepTable.RAPOR_GRUBU(satirNo) = "TESVÝYE/MONTAJ/DENEME"
			RepTable.RAPOR_ALT_GRUBU(satirNo) = "PRES ALTI ÇALIÞMASI"
			MesaiVar = True
			TalasliImalat = False
			PresAltiCalismasi = True
		Else
			RepTable.BOMREC_SIRANO(satirNo) = "06"
			RepTable.RAPOR_GRUBU(satirNo) = "TESVÝYE/MONTAJ/DENEME"
			RepTable.RAPOR_ALT_GRUBU(satirNo) = "TESVÝYE MONTAJ"
			MesaiVar = True
			TalasliImalat = False
		End if
		if Not MesaiVar Then
			MesaiSuresi = 0
		Else
			MesaiArr = GetMesaiSure(S20T.RECTARIH1(i), S20T.RECTIME1(i), S20T.RECTARIH2(i), S20T.RECTIME2(i), TalasliImalat)
			MesaiSuresi = MesaiArr(0) + MesaiArr(1)
		End if
		RepTable.OP_NO(satirNo) = OpNo
		RepTable.BOMREC_CODE(satirNo) = StokKodu
		RepTable.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE(satirNo) = "I"
		RepTable.EVRAKNO(satirNo) = S20T.EVRAKNO(i)
		RepTable.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(satirNo) = S20T.KOD(i)
		if PresAltiCalismasi Then
			if S20T.SURE(i) > 0 Then
				RepTable.BOMREC_KAYNAK0(satirNo) = S20T.SURE(i)' - MesaiSuresi
			Else
				RepTable.BOMREC_KAYNAK0(satirNo) = S20T.SURE(i)
			End if
			RepTable.BOMREC_KAYNAK1(satirNo) = S20T.TOPLAM_OPRTR_S_ST(i)
		Else
			RepTable.BOMREC_KAYNAK0(satirNo) = S20T.SURE(i) '- MesaiSuresi
			RepTable.BOMREC_KAYNAK1(satirNo) = S20T.TOPLAM_OPRTR_S_ST(i) '- MesaiSuresi
		End if
		RepTable.SF_UNIT(satirNo) = "SAAT"
'		Fiyat = Doc.StandartMaliyet_K("I", S20T.KOD(i),1,"SAAT", Doc.BuGun,"YL","YL", RaporPB)
		ToplamTezgahMaliyeti = GetToplamOperasyonMaliyeti(S20T.KOD(i), RepTable.BOMREC_KAYNAK0(satirNo), RepTable.BOMREC_KAYNAK1(satirNo), Doc.BuGun, "YL", RaporPB)
		RepTable.OR_FIYAT(satirNo) = 0
		RepTable.PRICEUNIT(satirNo) = RaporPB
		RepTable.OR_TUTAR(satirNo) = ToplamTezgahMaliyeti
		RepTable.RPB_TUTAR(satirNo) = ToplamTezgahMaliyeti
		RepTable.RPB_OPTUTAR(satirNo) = ToplamTezgahMaliyeti
		if MesaiSuresi > 0 and (not PresAltiCalismasi or (PresAltiCalismasi and S20T.TOPLAM_OPRTR_S_ST(i) = 0)) Then
			satirNo = RepTable.AddRow()
			if trim(GK4) = "TAL" Then
				RepTable.BOMREC_SIRANO(satirNo) = "05"
				RepTable.RAPOR_GRUBU(satirNo) = "TALAÞLI ÝMALAT"
				GrupAdi = Doc.GetGecoValue("I00GK5", "AD", GK5, -1)
				RepTable.RAPOR_ALT_GRUBU(satirNo) = trim(GrupAdi) & " FAZLA MESAÝ EK MALÝYETÝ"
				RepTable.BOMREC_ALT_SIRANO(satirNo) = trim(Doc.GetGecoValue("I00GK5", "AD_L2", GK5, -1))
				MesaiVar = True
				RepTable.SURE_HARIC(satirNo) = "E"
			Elseif trim(GK4) = "KALIP DENE" Then
				RepTable.BOMREC_SIRANO(satirNo) = "06"
				RepTable.RAPOR_GRUBU(satirNo) = "TESVÝYE/MONTAJ/DENEME"
				RepTable.RAPOR_ALT_GRUBU(satirNo) = "PRES ALTI ÇALIÞMASI FAZLA MESAÝ EK MALÝYETÝ"
				MesaiVar = True
				RepTable.SURE_HARIC(satirNo) = "E"
			Else
				RepTable.BOMREC_SIRANO(satirNo) = "06"
				RepTable.RAPOR_GRUBU(satirNo) = "TESVÝYE/MONTAJ/DENEME"
				RepTable.RAPOR_ALT_GRUBU(satirNo) = "TESVÝYE MONTAJ FAZLA MESAÝ EK MALÝYETÝ"
				MesaiVar = True
				RepTable.SURE_HARIC(satirNo) = "E"
			End if
			
			RepTable.OP_NO(satirNo) = OpNo
			RepTable.BOMREC_CODE(satirNo) = StokKodu
			RepTable.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE(satirNo) = "I"
			RepTable.EVRAKNO(satirNo) = S20T.EVRAKNO(i)
			RepTable.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(satirNo) = S20T.KOD(i)
			RepTable.BOMREC_KAYNAK0(satirNo) = MesaiArr(0) + MesaiArr(1)
			RepTable.BOMREC_KAYNAK1(satirNo) = MesaiArr(0) + MesaiArr(1)
			RepTable.SF_UNIT(satirNo) = "SAAT"
			IscilikFiyati = GetUnsurOpMl(S20T.KOD(i), "YL", "DÝ", Doc.BuGun, RaporPB)
			RepTable.OR_FIYAT(satirNo) = MesaiArr(0) * IscilikFiyati * 0.5 + MesaiArr(1) * IscilikFiyati
			RepTable.PRICEUNIT(satirNo) = RaporPB
			RepTable.OR_TUTAR(satirNo) = MesaiArr(0) * IscilikFiyati * 0.5 + MesaiArr(1) * IscilikFiyati
			RepTable.RPB_TUTAR(satirNo) = MesaiArr(0) * IscilikFiyati * 0.5 + MesaiArr(1) * IscilikFiyati
			RepTable.RPB_OPTUTAR(satirNo) = MesaiArr(0) * IscilikFiyati * 0.5 + MesaiArr(1) * IscilikFiyati
		End if
	Next
End Sub
Sub SendTableToExcel(Tablo)
	Set pe2 = Doc.CreateSObject("ExcelOutput")
	PE2.SetOrientation "P"
	PE2.PageB_ReportHead_std1 Tablo.GetAppName
	
	Alanlar = Tablo.GetFields()
	ncol = 0
	For i=0 To uBound(Alanlar,1)
		AlanAdi = Alanlar(i,0)
		if Cikacak(AlanAdi) Then
			ncol = ncol+1
			if ncol > 256 Then Exit For
			AlanTuru = Alanlar(i,2)
			If AlanTuru = "INT16" or AlanTuru = "INT32" or AlanTuru = "DOUBLE" Then
				Align = "RIGHT"
			Else
				Align = "LEFT"
			End if
			pe2.AddColumnDef Alanlar(i,0), Alanlar(i,3), Alanlar(i,4), Align
			pe2.SetRowValue_String -1, AlanAdi
		End if
	Next
	PE2.LineOut
	
	For satirNo = 1 to Tablo.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Excel 'e gönderiliyor : " & Tablo.GetAppName & " - " & i & " / " & Tablo.GetRowCount
		PE2.InitRowValues
		
		ExcelSatir = -1
		ncol = 0
		For i=0 To uBound(Alanlar,1)
			AlanAdi = Alanlar(i,0)
			if Cikacak(AlanAdi) Then
				ncol = ncol+1
				if ncol > 256 Then Exit For
				AlanTuru = Alanlar(i,2)
				If AlanTuru = "INT16" or AlanTuru = "INT32" or AlanTuru = "DOUBLE" Then
					Deger = Tablo.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi,satirNo)
					pe2.SetRowValue_Double ExcelSatir, Deger
				ElseIf AlanTuru = "DATE" Then
					OutputStr=Doc.StrToNorDate(Tablo.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi,satirNo))
					pe2.SetRowValue_String ExcelSatir,OutputStr
				Else
					pe2.SetRowValue_String ExcelSatir,Tablo.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi,satirNo)
				End if
			End if
		Next
		PE2.LineOut
	Next
	pe2.Show
End Sub
Function Cikacak(AlanAdi)
	Cikacak = True
	if InStr(1, AlanAdi, "REPORT__") > 0 Then
		Cikacak = False
	End if
	if InStr(1, AlanAdi, "BOMREC_CODE_") > 0 Then
		Cikacak = False
	End if
End Function
Sub HammaddeleriOku(StokKodu, OpNo, RepTable, RaporPB)
	QStr = "SF_VRI_VR_R1>='" & trim(StokKodu) & "'&SF_VRI_VR_R1<='" & trim(StokKodu) & "zzzz'"
	Set S29T = Doc.Select1("d4f44gg", "STOK29T", QStr, "", -1)
	Call FiyatiOlmayanIrsaliyelereSiparistenFiyatGetir(Doc, S29T)
	
	OncekiFirma = Doc.Customp_Gets("_DV_OFR")
	Set Doc_Old = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI2(Doc.GetDocType, "", OncekiFirma)
	Set S29T_Old = Doc_Old.Select1("dX4f44gg", "STOK29T", QStr, "", -1)
	Call FiyatiOlmayanIrsaliyelereSiparistenFiyatGetir(Doc_Old, S29T_Old)
	if S29T_Old.GetRowCount() > 0 Then S29T.AppendTable(S29T_Old)
	
	For i = 1 to S29T.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Satýnalma Kayýtlarý Okunuyor : " & i & " / " & S29T.GetRowCount()
		if Left(S29T.KOD(i), 8) <> "3050 K60" Then
			satirNo = RepTable.AddRow()
			GK8 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "GK_8", S29T.KOD(i), "")
			if Left(S29T.KOD(i), 7) = "3050 K0" Then
				RepTable.BOMREC_SIRANO(satirNo) = "03"
				RepTable.RAPOR_GRUBU(satirNo) = "DÖKÜM"
				RepTable.RAPOR_ALT_GRUBU(satirNo) = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", S29T.KOD(i), "")
			Elseif trim(GK8) = "KALIP TAS" Then
				RepTable.BOMREC_SIRANO(satirNo) = "01"
				RepTable.RAPOR_GRUBU(satirNo) = "CAD/CAM/PROJE TAKIP"
				RepTable.RAPOR_ALT_GRUBU(satirNo) = Doc.GetGecoValue("STKGK8", "AD", GK8, -1)
			Else
				RepTable.BOMREC_SIRANO(satirNo) = "04"
				RepTable.RAPOR_GRUBU(satirNo) = "STANDART MALZEME"
				AnaKod = Doc.Kod_SeviyeAl(S29T.KOD(i), -1)
				RepTable.RAPOR_ALT_GRUBU(satirNo) = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", AnaKod, "")
			End if
			RepTable.OP_NO(satirNo) = OpNo
			RepTable.BOMREC_CODE(satirNo) = StokKodu
			RepTable.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE(satirNo) = "H"
			RepTable.EVRAKNO(satirNo) = S29T.EVRAKNO(i)
			RepTable.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(satirNo) = S29T.KOD(i)
			RepTable.BOMREC_KAYNAK0(satirNo) = S29T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
			RepTable.SF_UNIT(satirNo) = S29T.SF_SF_UNIT(i)
			RepTable.OR_FIYAT(satirNo) = S29T.OR_FIYAT(i)
			RepTable.PRICEUNIT(satirNo) = S29T.PUNIT_ORG(i)
			RepTable.OR_TUTAR(satirNo) = S29T.OR_TUTAR(i)
			Kur = Doc.GetKurN(S29T.PUNIT_ORG(i), RaporPB, Doc.BuGun, "", -1)
			RepTable.RPB_TUTAR(satirNo) = S29T.OR_TUTAR(i) * Kur
			RepTable.RPB_OPTUTAR(satirNo) = 0
		End if
	Next
End Sub
Sub FasonMasraflariniOku(StokKodu, OpNo, RepTable, RaporPB)
	Set S68T = Doc.Select1("d4f44gg", "STOK68T", "SF_VRI_VR_R1>='" & trim(StokKodu) & "'&SF_VRI_VR_R1<='" & trim(StokKodu) & "zzzz'", "", -1)

	if S68T_Old.GetRowCount() > 0 Then S68T.AppendTable(S68T_Old)
	
	' msgbox S68T.GetRowCount

		satirNo = RepTable.AddRow
		RepTable.BOMREC_SIRANO(satirNo) = "05"
		RepTable.RAPOR_GRUBU(satirNo) = "TALAÞLI ÝMALAT"
		RepTable.RAPOR_ALT_GRUBU(satirNo) = "FASON ÝÞLEME"
		RepTable.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE(satirNo) = "F"
		RepTable.BOMREC_ALT_SIRANO(satirNo) = "99"
	End if
	
	For i = 1 to S68T.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Satýnalma Kayýtlarý Okunuyor : " & i & " / " & S68T.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = RepTable.AddRow()
		RepTable.OP_NO(satirNo) = OpNo
		RepTable.BOMREC_CODE(satirNo) = StokKodu
'		if Left(S68T.KOD(i), 8) = "2050 K10" or t

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : YILDIZ_2014 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : FIYAT1

Dim S48, Doc_Old, TezgahSaatUcreti
Sub Makro1()
	MsgBox  GetFiyat("K70 150", "", Doc.BuGun)
End Sub
Function GetFiyat(StokKodu, FOKodu, Tarih)
'	Doc.d7MsgBox "Stok Kodu : " & StokKodu, "", MB_OK

	TezgahSaatUcreti = 0.35
	Doc.CreateTableObject "XXXX=STOK48T", "TMPCOPY"
	Set S48 = Doc.GetTableObject("XXXX")
	
	OncekiFirma = Doc.Customp_Gets("_DV_OFR")
	Set Doc_Old = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI2("STOK21", "", OncekiFirma)

	GetFiyat = GetTLFiyat(StokKodu, FOKodu, Tarih)

	S48.GroupBy("KOD")
	For i = 1 to S48.GetRowCount()
		MsgStr = MsgStr & S48.KOD(i) & " " & Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", S48.KOD(i), "") & " fiyat bulunamadý." & vbcrlf
	Next
	if trim(MsgStr) <> "" Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Satýnalma Fiyat Listelerinde fiyatý bulunamayan kodlar " & vbcrlf & MsgStr, "", MB_OK
	End if
End Function
Function GetTLFiyat(StokKodu, FOKodu, Tarih)
	ret=Doc.Calc_Bom("M","M", StokKodu, 1, 1, "", FOKodu, "", "", "", "", "", "") 
	Set Tbmrsys1 = Doc.GetTableObject("_WBMRSYS_SELECT")

	TabloStr = "D" & Replace(CStr(Timer * Rnd), ".", "")

	Doc.CreateTableObject TabloStr & "=WBMRSYS", "TMPCOPY"
	Set Tbmrsys = Doc.GetTableobject(TabloStr)
	Tbmrsys.CopyTable(Tbmrsys1)

	if Tbmrsys.GetRowCount = 0 Then
		if Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "KART_TIPI", StokKodu, "") = "F" Then
			GetTLFiyat = GetToplamFasonFiyati(StokKodu, Tarih)
		Else
			GetTLFiyat = GetSatinalmaTLFiyati(StokKodu, Tarih)
		End if
		Doc.DeleteTableObject TabloStr
		Exit Function
	End if
	TLFiyat = 0
	For i = 1 to Tbmrsys.GetRowCount()
		if Tbmrsys.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE(i) = "H" Then
			if Tbmrsys.IALTSEVIYEBULUNDU(i) = 0 Then
				if Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "KART_TIPI", Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i), "") = "F" Then
					TLFiyat = TLFiyat + GetToplamFasonFiyati(Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i), Tarih) * Tbmrsys.KAYNAKMIKTART(i)
				Else
					TLFiyat = TLFiyat + GetSatinalmaTLFiyati(Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i), Tarih) * Tbmrsys.KAYNAKMIKTART(i)
				End if
			Else
				TLFiyat = TLFiyat + GetSonFasonFiyat(Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i)) * Tbmrsys.KAYNAKMIKTART(i)
			End if
		Else
			if Left(Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i), 1) <> "F" Then
				TLFiyat = TLFiyat + Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAK0(i) * TezgahSaatUcreti
			End if
		End if
	Next
	GetTLFiyat = TLFiyat
	Doc.DeleteTableObject TabloStr
	Doc.DeleteTableObject "_WBMRSYS_SELECT"
End Function
Function GetSatinalmaTLFiyati(StokKodu, Tarih)
	GetSatinalmaTLFiyati = 0
	TabloStr = "ddrr"
	Set S48T = Doc.Select1(TabloStr, "STOK48T", "KULLANIM_YERI='2'&KOD='"+StokKodu+"'", "", -1)
	if S48T.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		Kur = Doc.GetKurN(S48T.PRICEUNIT(1), Doc.DefaultPB, Tarih, "", -1)
		Birim = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "IUNIT", StokKodu, "")
		BirimDonusumu = Doc.Stok00_toUnit(StokKodu, Birim, S48T.SF_UNIT(1), -1)
		GetSatinalmaTLFiyati = S48T.PRICE(1) * Kur * BirimDonusumu
		Exit Function
	End if
	GetSatinalmaTLFiyati = GetSonSatinalmaFiyat(StokKodu)
	if GetSatinalmaTLFiyati = 0 Then
		ss = S48.AddRow
		S48.KOD(ss) = StokKodu
	End if
	Doc.DeleteTableObject TabloStr
End Function
Function GetSonSatinalmaFiyat(StokKodu)
	GetSonSatinalmaFiyat = 0
	TabloStr5 = "sswweeqq"
	Set S68T = Doc.Select1(TabloStr5, "STOK10A", "EVRAKTYPE='SI'&KOD='"+trim(StokKodu)+"'&TL_TUTAR>0", "<TARIH", 1)
	if S68T.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		GetSonSatinalmaFiyat = Doc.Div(S68T.TL_TUTAR(1)+S68T.TL_NONMIKTARTUTAR(1), S68T.SF_MIKTAR(1))
	End if
	if GetSonSatinalmaFiyat = 0 Then
		GetSonSatinalmaFiyat = GetOncekiSatinalmaFiyat(StokKodu)
	End if
	Doc.DeleteTableObject TabloStr5
End Function
Function GetOncekiSatinalmaFiyat(StokKodu)
	GetOncekiSatinalmaFiyat = 0
	TabloStr5 = "sswweeqq"
	Set S68T = Doc_Old.Select1(TabloStr5, "STOK10A", "EVRAKTYPE='SI'&KOD='"+trim(StokKodu)+"'&TL_TUTAR>0", "<TARIH", 1)
	if S68T.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		GetOncekiSatinalmaFiyat = Doc.Div(S68T.TL_TUTAR(1)+S68T.TL_NONMIKTARTUTAR(1), S68T.SF_MIKTAR(1))
	Else
		GetOncekiSatinalmaFiyat = GetAcilisFisiFiyat(StokKodu)
	End if
	Doc.DeleteTableObject TabloStr5
End Function
Function GetSonFasonFiyat(StokKodu)
	GetSonFasonFiyat = 0
	TabloStr5 = "sswweeqq"
	Set S68T = Doc.Select1(TabloStr5, "STOK10A", "EVRAKTYPE='FG'&KOD='"+trim(StokKodu)+"'&TL_TUTAR>0", "<TARIH", 1)
	if S68T.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
'		GetSonFasonFiyat = Doc.Div(S68T.TL_TUTAR(1)+S68T.TL_NONMIKTARTUTAR(1), S68T.SF_MIKTAR(1))
		GetSonFasonFiyat = Doc.Div(S68T.TL_NONMIKTARTUTAR(1), S68T.SF_MIKTAR(1))
	End if
	if GetSonFasonFiyat = 0 Then
		GetSonFasonFiyat = GetOncekiFasonFiyat(StokKodu)
	End if
	Doc.DeleteTableObject TabloStr5
End Function
Function GetOncekiFasonFiyat(StokKodu)
	GetOncekiFasonFiyat = 0
	TabloStr5 = "sswweeqq"
	Set S68T = Doc_Old.Select1(TabloStr5, "STOK10A", "EVRAKTYPE='FG'&KOD='"+trim(StokKodu)+"'&TL_NONMIKTARTUTAR>0", "<TARIH", 1)
	if S68T.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		GetOncekiFasonFiyat = Doc.Div(S68T.TL_NONMIKTARTUTAR(1), S68T.SF_MIKTAR(1))
	End if
	Doc.DeleteTableObject TabloStr5
End Function
Function GetToplamFasonFiyati(StokKodu, Tarih)
	TabloStr4 = "ddSD34456y66"
	Set S68T = Doc.Select1(TabloStr4, "STOK68T", "KOD='" & StokKodu & "'", "<TARIH", -1)
	if S68T.GetRowCount() = 0  Then
		GetToplamFasonFiyati = 0
		Doc.DeleteTableObject TabloStr4
		Exit Function
	End if

	EvrakNo = S68T.EVRAKNO
	KarsiTrNum = S68T.KARSITRNUM
	TabloStr2 = "Z" & Replace(CStr(Timer * Rnd), ".", "")
	Set S68T = Doc.Select1(TabloStr2, "STOK68T", "KOD='" & StokKodu & "'&EVRAKNO='" & S68T.EVRAKNO & "'&KARSITRNUM=" & S68T.KARSITRNUM & "'", "", -1)
	S68T.GroupBy("KARSITRNUM")
	MamulMiktar = S68T.SF_MIKTAR
	FasonTutar = S68T.TL_TUTAR
	TabloStr1 = "X" & Replace(CStr(Timer * Rnd), ".", "")
	Set S68TI = Doc.Select1(TabloStr1, "STOK68TI", "EVRAKNO='" & EvrakNo & "'&KARSITRNUM=" & KarsiTrNum & "'", "", -1)
	For i = 1 to S68TI.GetRowCount()
		MalzemeFiyati = GetTLFiyat(S68TI.KOD(i), "", Tarih)
		ToplamTutar = ToplamTutar + MalzemeFiyati * S68TI.SF_MIKTAR(i)
	Next
	GetToplamFasonFiyati = Doc.Div(ToplamTutar + FasonTutar, MamulMiktar)
	Doc.DeleteTableObject TabloStr1
	Doc.DeleteTableObject TabloStr4
End Function
Function GetAcilisFisiFiyat(StokKodu)
	GetAcilisFisiFiyat = 0
	TabloStr5 = "wwwehhhg66667"
	Set S10A = Doc.Select1(TabloStr5, "STOK10A", "EVRAKTYPE='0S'&KOD='"+trim(StokKodu)+"'&TL_TUTAR>0", "", 1)
	if S10A.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		GetAcilisFisiFiyat = Doc.Div(S10A.TL_TUTAR(1), S10A.SF_MIKTAR(1))
	End if
	Doc.DeleteTableObject TabloStr5
End Function

Sub Makro1()
	Set fff = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")
	for i = 1 to STOK24T.GetRecCount()
		if trim(STOK24T.KOD(i)) <> "" then
'			Maliyet = Doc.SonHesaplanMaliyet(STOK24T.KOD(i),STOK24E.AMBCODE,STOK24E.TARIH)
			Redim A(7)
			A(0)=STOK24T.KOD(i)
			Tarih = STOK24E.TARIH
			If Tarih < "2005/05/04" Then Tarih = "2005/05/04"
'			Maliyet = Doc.StandartMaliyet(A,1,STOK24T.SF_SF_UNIT(i),Tarih,"TK","TK",Doc.DefaultPB)
			StokKodu = STOK24T.KOD(i)
			FOKodu = ""
			Tarih = STOK24E.TARIH
			Maliyet = Doc.RunMacro3("MAKR7S.FIYAT", "GetFiyat", StokKodu, FOKodu, Tarih)
			STOK24T.SetCurrentRow(i)
			STOK24T.OR_FIYAT(i) = Doc.Round(Maliyet,6)
			STOK24T.PUNIT_ORG(i) = Doc.DefaultPB
		end if
	Next
End Sub

Sub Makro1()
	Set Excel = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
	Set Wb1 = Excel.Workbooks.Open("c:\demirbas.xls")
	Set Sh1 = Wb1.Sheets(1)
	For i = 2 to 65000
		if trim(Sh1.Cells(i,1).Value) = "" Then Exit For
		Doc.StatusBarMessage i
		Doc.Load_Voucher Sh1.Cells(i,1).Value
		DMRB60E.AD = Sh1.Cells(i,2).Value
		Gk1 = GKYoksaEkle(trim(Sh1.Cells(i,3).Value), "DMRGK4", "")
'		Gk2 = GKYoksaEkle(trim(Sh1.Cells(i,4).Value), "DMRGK2", Sh1.Cells(i,5).Value)
		DMRB60E.GK_4 = Gk4
'		DMRB60E.GK_2 = Gk2
		DMRB60E.MUHASEBEKOD = Sh1.Cells(i,4).Value
		DMRB60E.MUHASEBEKODG = Sh1.Cells(i,5).Value
		DMRB60E.AMT_AYIRMA_YONTEMI = Sh1.Cells(i,6).Value
		DMRB60E.AMT_ORANI = Sh1.Cells(i,7).Value
		DMRB60E.AMT_AYIRMA_KISMI_MI = CStr(Sh1.Cells(i,9).Value)
		DMRB60E.AA_KISMI_GA = CStr(Sh1.Cells(i,10).Value)
		DMRB60E.AMTA_TABI_BEDEL = CStr(Sh1.Cells(i,11).Value)
		DMRB60E.AA_SATISYILINDA_HGA = CStr(Sh1.Cells(i,12).Value)
		DMRB60E.AA_OMUR_16 = CStr(Sh1.Cells(i,13).Value)
		DMRB60E.CHKOD_ALIS = GetCHKodu(CStr(Sh1.Cells(i,14).Value))
		DMRB60E.SATINALMA_TARIH = Getd7Tarih(Sh1.Cells(i,16).Value)
		DMRB60E.SATINALMA_TUTAR = Doc.CDbl(Sh1.Cells(i,17).Value)
		DMRB60E.SATINALMA_OR_TUTAR = Doc.CDbl(Sh1.Cells(i,18).Value)
		DMRB60E.SATINALMA_OR_KUR = Doc.CDbl(Sh1.Cells(i,19).Value)
		DMRB60E.SATINALMA_ACIKLAMA = CStr(Sh1.Cells(i,20).Value)
		DMRB60E.SATINALMA_XEVRAKNO = CStr(Sh1.Cells(i,21).Value)
		DMRB60E.CHKOD_SATIS = GetCHKodu(CStr(Sh1.Cells(i,22).Value))
		DMRB60E.SATIS_TARIH = Getd7Tarih(Sh1.Cells(i,24).Value)
		DMRB60E.SATIS_TUTAR = Doc.CDbl(Sh1.Cells(i,25).Value)
		DMRB60E.DVR_YIL = Sh1.Cells(i,26).Value
		DMRB60E.DVR_DO_SKB = Sh1.Cells(i,27).Value
		DMRB60E.DVR_DO_AMT_AY = Sh1.Cells(i,28).Value
		DMRB60E.DVR_DO_AMT_NAY = Sh1.Cells(i,29).Value

		Doc.Save_Voucher
	Next
	Doc.LoadEmpty_Voucher
	Wb1.Close
	Excel.Quit
End Sub
Function GKYoksaEkle(Kod, GrupKodu, Ad)
	Set GST = Doc.Select1("ddee", "GECOUST", "EVRAKNO='" & GrupKodu & "'&KOD='" & Kod & "'", "", -1)
	if GST.GetRowCount = 0 Then
		Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("GECO10", "")
		Set GT = Doc1.GetTableObject("GECOUST")
		Doc1.Load_Voucher GrupKodu
		satirNo = GT.AddRow
		GT.KOD(satirNo) = Kod
		GT.AD(satirNo) = Ad
		Doc1.Save_Voucher
		Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
	End if
	GKYoksaEkle = Kod
End Function
Function Getd7Tarih(ATarih)
	Getd7Tarih = Right("00" & Day(ATarih), 2) & "/" & Right("00" & Month(ATarih), 2) & "/" & Year(ATarih)
End Function
Function GetCHKodu(CHKodu)
	GetCHKodu = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "KOD", Doc.Leftjustify(trim(CHKodu), 16), "")
End Function

Sub Makro1()
Set S48T = Doc.Select1("de12","STOK48T","","",-1)
for i = 1 to FIYATFARKI_T.GetRecCount()
	Set S48T = Doc.Select1("de12","STOK48T","EVRAKNO= '"& FIYATFARKI_E.FIYATLISTESI1 &"' & KOD='"& FIYATFARKI_T.KOD(i) &"' & KRI_TARIH_1 <= '"& FIYATFARKI_T.TARIH(i) &"'","<KRI_TARIH_1",-1)
	if S48T.GetRecCount() > 0 then
		FIYATFARKI_T.YENI_FIYAT(i) = S48T.PRICE(1)
		FIYATFARKI_T.SetCurrentRow(i)
	End if
Next
End Sub                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            

Ekran Formu
Uygulamalar Ýçin Baþlangýç Ekran Formu Ayarlarý
Baþlangýç Ekran Formu Bu Uygulama için : 
Bu Uygulama için Baþlangýç Ekran Formu :

Yeni Alt Ekra Form Kodu: 
Alt Ekran Açýlmadan Önce Çalýþtýrýlacak Makro : 


----------------------------------------------------------------
Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : YILDIZ_2014 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : BEYANNAME
Hangi Tablo için : EBEYNM2
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : ÝHRAÇ KAYITLI SATIÞ FATURASI LÝSTESÝ
Kod : 
Option Explicit

Dim FileName
Dim SheetName
Dim FileName2
Dim SheetName2

const xlAutomatic=-4105
const xlCenter=-4108
const xlLeft=-4131
const xlRight=-4152
const xlContext=-5002
const xlContinuous=1
const xlThin=2

const xlEdgeBottom=9
const xlEdgeLeft=7
const xlEdgeRight=10
const xlEdgeTop=8

FileName="C:\Listeler\Ihrac Kayitli Satis Faturalari\Ihrac Kayitli Satis Fatura Listesi.xls"
SheetName="Ihrac Kayitli Satis Faturalari"

Sub Makro1()
	
	Dim i
	Dim Excel
	Dim Wb
	Dim Sh
	
	Dim cpt
	cpt=Doc.Customp_Gets("EBYNDOSD")
	cpt=Trim(cpt)
	if cpt<>"" then
		FileName=RePlace(FileName,"C:\Listeler\",cpt)
		if CreateIfNotExistDir(cpt+"Ihrac Kayitli Satis Faturalari") then
			Exit Sub
		end if
	else
		if CreateIfNotExistDir("C:\Listeler") then
			Exit Sub
		end if
		if CreateIfNotExistDir("C:\Listeler\Ihrac Kayitli Satis Faturalari") then
			Exit Sub
		end if
	end if
	
	Doc.StatusbarMessage "Excel Açiliyor"
	Set Excel = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
	Set Wb = Excel.Workbooks.Add
	Set Sh = Wb.Sheets(1)
	Sh.Name = SheetName
	
	Dim DeleteSheets()
	ReDim DeleteSheets(Wb.Sheets.Count)
	
	For i = 1 To Wb.Sheets.Count
		If Trim(Wb.Sheets(i).Name) <> SheetName Then
			DeleteSheets(i) = Wb.Sheets(i).Name
		End If
	Next
	
	For i = 1 To UBound(DeleteSheets)
		If Trim(DeleteSheets(i)) <> "" Then
			Wb.Sheets(DeleteSheets(i)).Delete
		End If
	Next
	
	Call SetHeader(Sh)
	Call SetData(Sh)
	
	Call DeleteFile(FileName)
	
	Excel.ActiveWorkbook.SaveAs FileName
	
	Excel.Quit
	Set Excel=nothing
	
	Doc.d7MsgBox "Oluþturulan Dosya-> "+FileName,"Freedom",MB_OK
	
	Dim tbl_EBEYNM2E
	Set tbl_EBEYNM2E=Doc.GetTableObject("EBEYNM2E")
	tbl_EBEYNM2E.XLSFILENAME1=FileName
	
End Sub
Sub SetHeader(Sh)
	
	Call WriteCel(Sh,2, 8, 18, 3.71,  "Arial", 14, True,False,xlCenter,"","ÝHRAÇ KAYITLI SATIÞ FATURASI LiSTESÝ")
	
	Call WriteCel(Sh,4, 2, 76.75, 3.71,  "Arial", 10, True, True, xlCenter,"","Sýra No")
	Call WriteCel(Sh,4, 3, 76.75, 11.86, "Arial", 10, True, True, xlCenter,"","Ýhraç Kayýtlý Satýþ Faturasýnýn Tarihi")
	Call WriteCel(Sh,4, 4, 76.75, 7.86,  "Arial", 10, True, True, xlCenter,"","Ýhraç Kayýtlý Satýþ Faturasýnýn Serisi")
	Call WriteCel(Sh,4, 5, 76.75, 10.29, "Arial", 10, True, True, xlCenter,"","Ýhraç Kayýtlý Satýþ Faturasýnýn Sira No'su")
	Call WriteCel(Sh,4, 6, 76.75, 20, "Arial", 10, True, True, xlCenter,"","Alýcýnýn Adý-Soyadý / Ünvaný")
	Call WriteCel(Sh,4, 7, 76.75, 14,  "Arial", 10, True, True, xlCenter,"","Alýcýnýn Vergi Kimlik Numarasý / TC Kimlik Numarasý")
	Call WriteCel(Sh,4, 8, 76.75, 16.29, "Arial", 10, True, True, xlCenter,"","Malýn Cinsi")
	Call WriteCel(Sh,4, 9, 76.75, 11.14, "Arial", 10, True, True, xlCenter,"","Malýn Miktarý")
	Call WriteCel(Sh,4,10, 76.75, 12,  "Arial", 10, True, True, xlCenter,"","Malýn KDV Hariç Tutarý")
	Call WriteCel(Sh,4,11, 76.75, 8.43,  "Arial", 10, True, True, xlCenter,"","Malýn KDV'si")
	Call WriteCel(Sh,4,12, 76.75, 15,  "Arial", 10, True, True, xlCenter,"","Ýhracatçý Tarafýndan Yurt Dýþýna Düzenlenen Satýþ Faturasýnýn Tarihi(GÇB Üzerindeki Bilgiler)")
	Call WriteCel(Sh,4,13, 76.75, 13.57, "Arial", 10, True, True, xlCenter,"","GÇB Tescil No")
End Sub

Sub SetData(Sh)
	Dim i
	Dim tbl_EBEYNM21
	Dim FT_IVD_KH_TOPLAM
	Dim FT_KDV_TUTARI
	Dim Qstr,S70T
	Dim Donembasi,donemsonu
	' Set tbl_EBEYNM21=Doc.GetTableObject("EBEYNM21")
	
	Donembasi = "01"& "/"& EBEYNM2E.IDB_AY &"/"& EBEYNM2E.IDB_YIL
	Donembasi = Doc.AyinilkGunu(Donembasi)
	Donemsonu = Doc.AyinSonGunu(Donembasi)
	
	Set tbl_EBEYNM21 = Doc.Select1G("ds1224","STOK70T","NITELIK='XK' & TARIH >= '"& Donembasi  &"' & TARIH <= '"& Donemsonu &"'","","EVRAKNO",-1)
	
	'    QStr = "SELECT STOK70T.* FROM STOK70T"
	'    QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN STOK70E ON STOK70T.EVRAKNO=STOK70E.EVRAKNO"
	'    QStr = QStr & " WHERE STOK70E.NITELIK='XK'"
	'    QStr = QStr & " GROUP BY EVRAKNO"
	
	'    Set S70T = Doc.RunSQLQuery("vedrg35h35hgtfhg", QStr)
	
	Dim tbl_EBEYNM2A,tbl_EBYNM222
	Set tbl_EBEYNM2A=Doc.GetTableObject("EBEYNM2A")
	Dim ftserino,ftsirano,chkodu, chkoduadi,vergino,KDVharic,KDV,gcbtarih,gcbno,KDVharic_TOPLAM, KDV_TUTARI
	Dim miktar,zz,sira_no,tanim,gcb1,gcb2,zzz,faturagosterimsayisi
	
	sira_no = 0
	
	for i=1 to tbl_EBEYNM21.GetRecCount()
		tbl_EBEYNM21.SetCurrentRow i
		
		faturagosterimsayisi = 1
		gcb1 = Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK70E",tbl_EBEYNM21.EVRAKNO(i),"GCBN",0)
		gcb2 = Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK70E",tbl_EBEYNM21.EVRAKNO(i),"GCBNA",0)
		
		if trim(gcb1) = "" then
			faturagosterimsayisi = 1
		Elseif trim(gcb1) <> "" and trim(gcb2) = "" then
			faturagosterimsayisi = 2
		Elseif trim(gcb2) <> "" then
			faturagosterimsayisi = 3
		End if
		
		for zzz=1 to faturagosterimsayisi
			
			'  sira_no = i
			sira_no = sira_no + 1
			
			ftserino = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK70E","OR_FATURASERINO",tbl_EBEYNM21.EVRAKNO(i),"")
			ftsirano = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK70E","OR_FATURASIRANO",tbl_EBEYNM21.EVRAKNO(i),"")
			chkodu = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK70E","CH_KODU",tbl_EBEYNM21.EVRAKNO(i),"")
			chkoduadi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00","AD",chkodu,"")
			vergino = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00","VERGINO",chkodu,"")
			miktar=""
			KDVharic = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK70E","TL_IVD_KH_TOPLAM",tbl_EBEYNM21.EVRAKNO(i),"")
			KDV = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK70E","TL_KDVLERTOPLAM",tbl_EBEYNM21.EVRAKNO(i),"")

			if zzz=1 then
				gcbtarih = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK70E","GCBTARIHI",tbl_EBEYNM21.EVRAKNO(i),"")
				gcbtarih = Doc.StrToNorDate(gcbtarih)
				gcbno = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK70E","GCBNO",tbl_EBEYNM21.EVRAKNO(i),"")
			elseif zzz=2 then
				gcbno = gcb1
				gcbtarih = Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK70E",tbl_EBEYNM21.EVRAKNO(i),"GCBT",0)
			elseif zzz=3 then
				gcbno = gcb2
				gcbtarih = Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK70E",tbl_EBEYNM21.EVRAKNO(i),"GCBTA",0)
			End if

			tanim = Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK70E",tbl_EBEYNM21.EVRAKNO(i),"URUN",0)
			
			Doc.StatusbarMessage sira_no & ". Satir Formatlanip Yaziliyor"
			
			
			Set tbl_EBYNM222 = Doc.Select1G("d20014","STOK70T","NITELIK='XK' & EVRAKNO = '"& tbl_EBEYNM21.EVRAKNO(i) &"'& TARIH >= '"& Donembasi  &"' & TARIH <= '"& Donemsonu &"'","","EVRAKNO+SF_SF_UNIT",-1)
			For zz = 1 to tbl_EBYNM222.GetRecCount()
				miktar = miktar &" "& tbl_EBYNM222.SF_MIKTAR(zz) &" "& tbl_EBYNM222.SF_SF_UNIT(zz)
			Next
			Call WriteCel(Sh,sira_no +4, 2, 12, 3.71, "Arial", 9, True, True, xlCenter,"",  AlanUzunluklari(sira_no, tbl_EBEYNM21,i,tbl_EBEYNM2A,"SIRANO",       "CFLAG2","L"))
			Call WriteCel(Sh,sira_no +4, 3, 12, 11.86, "Arial", 9, True, True, xlLeft,"dd.mm.yyyy",      tbl_EBEYNM21.TARIH)
			Call WriteCel(Sh,sira_no +4, 4, 12, 7.86, "Arial", 9, True, True, xlLeft,"@",   AlanUzunluklari(ftserino, tbl_EBEYNM21,i,tbl_EBEYNM2A,"FT_SERINO",       "CFLAG3","L"))
			Call WriteCel(Sh,sira_no +4, 5, 12, 10.29, "Arial", 9, True, True, xlLeft,"@",   AlanUzunluklari(ftsirano, tbl_EBEYNM21,i,tbl_EBEYNM2A,"FT_SIRANO",        "CFLAG4","L"))
			Call WriteCel(Sh,sira_no +4, 6, 12, 20,  "Arial", 9, True, True, xlLeft,"@",   AlanUzunluklari(chkoduadi, tbl_EBEYNM21,i,tbl_EBEYNM2A,"FT_SATICI_UNVAN",  "CFLAG5","L"))
			Call WriteCel(Sh,sira_no +4, 7, 12, 14,  "Arial", 9, True, True, xlLeft,"@",   AlanUzunluklari(vergino , tbl_EBEYNM21,i,tbl_EBEYNM2A,"FT_ARACI_FRM_VDNTCNO", "CFLAG6","L"))
			Call WriteCel(Sh,sira_no +4, 8, 12, 16.29, "Arial", 9, True, True, xlLeft,"@",   AlanUzunluklari(tanim,  tbl_EBEYNM21,i,tbl_EBEYNM2A,"FT_MAL_V_HIZMETCINSI", "CFLAG7","L"))
			Call WriteCel(Sh,sira_no +4, 9, 12, 11.14, "Arial", 9, True, True, xlLeft,"@",   AlanUzunluklari(miktar , tbl_EBEYNM21,i,tbl_EBEYNM2A,"FT_MAL_V_HIZMETMIKTR", "CFLAG8","L"))
			Call WriteCel(Sh,sira_no +4,10, 12, 15,  "Arial", 9, True, True, xlRight,"#,##0.00", AlanUzunluklari(KDVharic , tbl_EBEYNM21,i,tbl_EBEYNM2A,"FT_IVD_KH_TOPLAM",  "CFLAG9","R"))
			Call WriteCel(Sh,sira_no +4,11, 12, 15,  "Arial", 9, True, True, xlRight,"#,##0.00", AlanUzunluklari(KDV,  tbl_EBEYNM21,i,tbl_EBEYNM2A,"FT_KDV_TUTARI",  "CFLAG10","R"))
			Call WriteCel(Sh,sira_no +4,12, 12, 15,  "Arial", 9, True, True, xlCenter,"@",  AlanUzunluklari(gcbtarih , tbl_EBEYNM21,i,tbl_EBEYNM2A,"FT_TARIHI",   "CFLAG11","L"))
			Call WriteCel(Sh,sira_no +4,13, 12, 13.57, "Arial", 9, True, True, xlLeft,"@",   AlanUzunluklari(gcbno ,  tbl_EBEYNM21,i,tbl_EBEYNM2A,"FT_GCBNO",   "CFLAG12","L"))
		Next
		KDVharic_TOPLAM = KDVharic_TOPLAM + KDVharic
		KDV_TUTARI = KDV_TUTARI + KDV
		
	next
	
	tbl_EBEYNM21.SetCurrentRow 1
	
	FT_IVD_KH_TOPLAM=Doc.dRound(KDVharic_TOPLAM)
	FT_KDV_TUTARI=Doc.dRound(KDV_TUTARI)
	
	Dim Sum1
	Dim Sum2
	
	Sum1="=SUM(J5:J@)"
	Sum2="=SUM(K5:K@)"
	
'	Sum1=RePlace(Sum1,"J@","J"&i+4-1)
'	Sum2=RePlace(Sum2,"K@","K"&i+4-1)

	Sum1 = FT_IVD_KH_TOPLAM
	Sum2 = FT_KDV_TUTARI
	
	Call WriteCel(Sh,sira_no+5,  9, 12, 11.14,"Arial", 9, True, True, xlLeft,"","TOPLAM")
	Call WriteCel(Sh,sira_no+5, 10, 12, 15, "Arial", 9, True, True, xlRight,"#,##0.00",Sum1)
	Call WriteCel(Sh,sira_no+5, 11, 12, 15, "Arial", 9, True, True, xlRight,"#,##0.00",Sum2)
	
End Sub


Function CreateIfNotExistDir(vDr)
	Dim fs
	Dim newfolder
	CreateIfNotExistDir=False
	Set fs=CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
	if Not fs.FolderExists(vDr) then
		Set newfolder=fs.CreateFolder(vDr)
		if Not IsObject(newfolder) then
			CreateIfNotExistDir=TRUE
			Doc.d7MsgBox vDr + " Dizin Oluþturulamiyor.","Freedom",MB_OK
			Exit Function
		end if
	end if
	set fs=nothing
End Function

Sub WriteCel(ObjSheet,RowNum, ColNum, pRowHeight, pColumnWidth, FontName, FontSize, FontBold, pWrapText,pHorizontalAlignment,pNumberFormat,pAciklama)
	With ObjSheet.Cells(RowNum, ColNum)
	if Trim(pNumberFormat)<>"" then
		.NumberFormat = pNumberFormat
	end if
	'        .FormulaR1C1 = pAciklama
	.Formula = pAciklama
	'        .RowHeight = pRowHeight '76.75
	.HorizontalAlignment = pHorizontalAlignment 'xlCenter
	.VerticalAlignment = xlCenter
	.WrapText = pWrapText 'True
	.Orientation = 0
	.AddIndent = False
	.IndentLevel = 0
	.ShrinkToFit = False
	.ReadingOrder = xlContext
	.MergeCells = False
	.Font.Name = FontName '"Arial"
	.Font.Size = FontSize '9
	.Font.Bold = FontBold 'True
	.ColumnWidth = pColumnWidth '3.71
	End With
	With ObjSheet.Cells(RowNum, ColNum).Borders(xlEdgeLeft)
	.LineStyle = xlContinuous
	.Weight = xlThin
	.ColorIndex = xlAutomatic
	End With
	With ObjSheet.Cells(RowNum, ColNum).Borders(xlEdgeTop)
	.LineStyle = xlContinuous
	.Weight = xlThin
	.ColorIndex = xlAutomatic
	End With
	With ObjSheet.Cells(RowNum, ColNum).Borders(xlEdgeBottom)
	.LineStyle = xlContinuous
	.Weight = xlThin
	.ColorIndex = xlAutomatic
	End With
	With ObjSheet.Cells(RowNum, ColNum).Borders(xlEdgeRight)
	.LineStyle = xlContinuous
	.Weight = xlThin
	.ColorIndex = xlAutomatic
	End With
	
End Sub


Function AlanUzunluklari(XmlValue,tbl_T,i,tbl_L,LenFieldName,FlagFieldName,JusTify)
	Dim FieldLen
	Dim Fieldflag
	AlanUzunluklari="" & XmlValue
	AlanUzunluklari=Trim(AlanUzunluklari)
	AlanUzunluklari=RePlace(AlanUzunluklari,Space(2),Space(1))
	AlanUzunluklari=RePlace(AlanUzunluklari,Space(2),Space(1))
	
	if Len(AlanUzunluklari)<=0 then Exit Function
	
	FieldLen=tbl_L.GetFieldValue(LenFieldName,-1)
	Fieldflag=tbl_L.GetFieldValue(FlagFieldName,-1)
	
	if FieldLen=0 then Exit Function
	if Trim(Fieldflag)="1" then
		if JusTify="R" then
			AlanUzunluklari=Right(AlanUzunluklari,FieldLen)
		elseif JusTify="L" then
			AlanUzunluklari=Left(AlanUzunluklari,FieldLen)
		end if
		Exit Function
	end if
	
	if Len(AlanUzunluklari)>FieldLen then
		Doc.d7MsgBox i & ". Satir. Belirtilen Alan("+LenFieldName+") Uzunluðu=" & FieldLen & " Yazilmaya Çaliþilan Bilginin Uzunluðu=" & Len(AlanUzunluklari),"Freedom",MB_OK
		AlanUzunluklari=" "
	end if
End Function


Sub DeleteFile(FileName)
	dim fs
	Set fs=CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
	if fs.FileExists(FileName) then
		fs.DeleteFile(FileName)
	end if
	set fs=nothing
End Sub

Option Explicit

Dim FileName
Dim SheetName
Dim FileName2
Dim SheetName2

const xlAutomatic=-4105
const xlCenter=-4108
const xlLeft=-4131
const xlRight=-4152
const xlContext=-5002
const xlContinuous=1
const xlThin=2

const xlEdgeBottom=9
const xlEdgeLeft=7
const xlEdgeRight=10
const xlEdgeTop=8

FileName="C:\Listeler\Tevkifatli Satis Faturalari\Tevkifatli Satis Fatura Listesi.xls"
SheetName="Tevkifatli Satis Faturalari"

Sub Makro1()

	Dim i
	Dim Excel
	Dim Wb
	Dim Sh

	Dim cpt
	cpt=Doc.Customp_Gets("EBYNDOSD")
	cpt=Trim(cpt) 
	if cpt<>"" then
		FileName=RePlace(FileName,"C:\Listeler\",cpt)
		if CreateIfNotExistDir(cpt+"Tevkifatli Satis Faturalari") then
			Exit Sub
		end if
	else
		if CreateIfNotExistDir("C:\Listeler") then
			Exit Sub
		end if
		if CreateIfNotExistDir("C:\Listeler\Tevkifatli Satis Faturalari") then
			Exit Sub
		end if
	end if

	Doc.StatusbarMessage "Excel Açiliyor"
	Set Excel = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
	Set Wb = Excel.Workbooks.Add
	Set Sh = Wb.Sheets(1)
	Sh.Name = SheetName	

	Dim DeleteSheets()
	ReDim DeleteSheets(Wb.Sheets.Count)

	For i = 1 To Wb.Sheets.Count
	  If Trim(Wb.Sheets(i).Name) <> SheetName Then
			DeleteSheets(i) = Wb.Sheets(i).Name
	  End If
	Next

	For i = 1 To UBound(DeleteSheets)
	  If Trim(DeleteSheets(i)) <> "" Then
			Wb.Sheets(DeleteSheets(i)).Delete
	  End If
	Next	

	Call SetHeader(Sh)
	Call SetData(Sh)

	Call DeleteFile(FileName) 

	Excel.ActiveWorkbook.SaveAs FileName

	Excel.Quit
	Set Excel=nothing

	Doc.d7MsgBox "Oluþturulan Dosya-> "+FileName,"Freedom",MB_OK

	Dim tbl_EBEYNM2E
	Set tbl_EBEYNM2E=Doc.GetTableObject("EBEYNM2E")
	tbl_EBEYNM2E.XLSFILENAME1=FileName

End Sub
Sub SetHeader(Sh)

	Call WriteCel(Sh,2, 8, 18, 3.71,		"Arial", 14, True,False,xlCenter,"","TEVKÝFATLI SATIÞLARA ÝLÝÞKÝN SATIÞ FATURASI LÝSTESÝ")

	Call WriteCel(Sh,4, 2, 76.75, 3.71,		"Arial", 10, True, True, xlCenter,"","Sýra No")
	Call WriteCel(Sh,4, 3, 76.75, 11.86,	"Arial", 10, True, True, xlCenter,"","Satýþ Faturasýnýn Tarihi")
	Call WriteCel(Sh,4, 4, 76.75, 7.86,		"Arial", 10, True, True, xlCenter,"","Satýþ Faturasýnýn Serisi")
	Call WriteCel(Sh,4, 5, 76.75, 10.29,	"Arial", 10, True, True, xlCenter,"","Satýþ Faturasýnýn Sira No'su")
	Call WriteCel(Sh,4, 6, 76.75, 20,	"Arial", 10, True, True, xlCenter,"","Alýcýnýn Adý-Soyadý / Ünvaný")	
	Call WriteCel(Sh,4, 7, 76.75, 14,		"Arial", 10, True, True, xlCenter,"","Alýcýnýn Vergi Kimlik Numarasý / TC Kimlik Numarasý")	
	Call WriteCel(Sh,4, 8, 76.75, 16.29,	"Arial", 10, True, True, xlCenter,"","Satýlan Mal ve/veya Hizmetin Cinsi")	
	Call WriteCel(Sh,4, 9, 76.75, 11.14,	"Arial", 10, True, True, xlCenter,"","Satýlan Mal ve/veya Hizmetin Miktarý")
	Call WriteCel(Sh,4,10, 76.75, 12,		"Arial", 10, True, True, xlCenter,"","Satýlan Mal ve/veya Hizmetin KDV Hariç Tutarý")
	Call WriteCel(Sh,4,11, 76.75, 8.43,		"Arial", 10, True, True, xlCenter,"","KDV'si")
	Call WriteCel(Sh,4,12, 76.75, 15,		"Arial", 10, True, True, xlCenter,"","Yapýlan Tevkifat Tutarý")
End Sub

Sub SetData(Sh)
	Dim i
	Dim tbl_EBEYNM21
	Dim FT_IVD_KH_TOPLAM
	Dim FT_KDV_TUTARI
	Dim Qstr,S70T
	Dim Donembasi,donemsonu
'	Set tbl_EBEYNM21=Doc.GetTableObject("EBEYNM21")

	Donembasi = "01"& "/"& EBEYNM2E.IDB_AY &"/"& EBEYNM2E.IDB_YIL
	Donemsonu = Doc.AyinSonGunu(Donembasi)

	Set tbl_EBEYNM21 = Doc.Select1G("ds1224","STOK70T","NITELIK='Z3' & TARIH >= '"& Donembasi  &"' & TARIH <= '"& Donemsonu &"'","","EVRAKNO",-1)

'    QStr = "SELECT STOK70T.* FROM STOK70T"
'    QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN STOK70E ON STOK70T.EVRAKNO=STOK70E.EVRAKNO"
'    QStr = QStr & " WHERE STOK70E.NITELIK='Z3'"
'    QStr = QStr & " GROUP BY EVRAKNO"

'    Set S70T = Doc.RunSQLQuery("vedrg35h35hgtfhg", QStr) 

	
	Dim tbl_EBEYNM2A,tbl_EBYNM222
	Set tbl_EBEYNM2A=Doc.GetTableObject("EBEYNM2A")
	Dim ftserino,ftsirano,chkodu, chkoduadi,vergino,KDVharic,KDV,tevkifat,gcbno,KDVharic_TOPLAM,Tevkifat_TOPLAM, KDV_TUTARI,miktar,zz,sira_no

	for i=1 to tbl_EBEYNM21.GetRecCount()
		tbl_EBEYNM21.SetCurrentRow i

		sira_no = i
		ftserino = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK70E","OR_FATURASERINO",tbl_EBEYNM21.EVRAKNO(i),"")
		ftsirano = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK70E","OR_FATURASIRANO",tbl_EBEYNM21.EVRAKNO(i),"")
		chkodu = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK70E","CH_KODU",tbl_EBEYNM21.EVRAKNO(i),"")
		chkoduadi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00","AD",chkodu,"")
		vergino = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00","VERGINO",chkodu,"")
		miktar=""
		KDVharic = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK70E","TL_IVD_KH_TOPLAM",tbl_EBEYNM21.EVRAKNO(i),"")
		KDV = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK70E","TL_KDVLERTOPLAM",tbl_EBEYNM21.EVRAKNO(i),"")
		tevkifat = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK70E","TL_KESINTI1",tbl_EBEYNM21.EVRAKNO(i),"")


		Doc.StatusbarMessage i & ". Satir Formatlanip Yaziliyor"

'		AlanDegeri = AlanUzunluklari(ftserino,				tbl_EBEYNM21,i,tbl_EBEYNM2A,"FT_SERINO","CFLAG3","L")
'		Call WriteCel(Sh,i+4, 4, 12, 7.86,	"Arial", 9, True, True, xlLeft,"@",	AlanDegeri)

		Set tbl_EBYNM222 = Doc.Select1G("d20014","STOK70T","NITELIK='Z3' & EVRAKNO = '"& tbl_EBEYNM21.EVRAKNO(i) &"'& TARIH >= '"& Donembasi  &"' & TARIH <= '"& Donemsonu &"'","","EVRAKNO+SF_SF_UNIT",-1)
		For zz = 1 to tbl_EBYNM222.GetRecCount()
			miktar = miktar &" "& tbl_EBYNM222.SF_MIKTAR(zz) &" "& tbl_EBYNM222.SF_SF_UNIT(zz)
		Next
		Call WriteCel(Sh,i+4, 2, 12, 3.71,	"Arial", 9, True, True, xlCenter,"",		AlanUzunluklari(sira_no,	tbl_EBEYNM21,i,tbl_EBEYNM2A,"SIRANO",	    		"CFLAG2","L"))
		Call WriteCel(Sh,i+4, 3, 12, 11.86,	"Arial", 9, True, True, xlLeft,"dd.mm.yyyy",					tbl_EBEYNM21.TARIH)
		Call WriteCel(Sh,i+4, 4, 12, 7.86,	"Arial", 9, True, True, xlLeft,"@",			AlanUzunluklari(ftserino,	tbl_EBEYNM21,i,tbl_EBEYNM2A,"FT_SERINO",	    		"CFLAG3","L"))
		Call WriteCel(Sh,i+4, 5, 12, 10.29,	"Arial", 9, True, True, xlLeft,"@",			AlanUzunluklari(ftsirano,	tbl_EBEYNM21,i,tbl_EBEYNM2A,"FT_SIRANO",      		"CFLAG4","L"))
		Call WriteCel(Sh,i+4, 6, 12, 20,		"Arial", 9, True, True, xlLeft,"@",			AlanUzunluklari(chkoduadi,	tbl_EBEYNM21,i,tbl_EBEYNM2A,"FT_SATICI_UNVAN",		"CFLAG5","L"))
		Call WriteCel(Sh,i+4, 7, 12, 14,		"Arial", 9, True, True, xlLeft,"@",			AlanUzunluklari(vergino ,	tbl_EBEYNM21,i,tbl_EBEYNM2A,"FT_ARACI_FRM_VDNTCNO",	"CFLAG6","L"))
		Call WriteCel(Sh,i+4, 8, 12, 16.29,	"Arial", 9, True, True, xlLeft,"@",			AlanUzunluklari("",		tbl_EBEYNM21,i,tbl_EBEYNM2A,"FT_MAL_V_HIZMETCINSI",	"CFLAG7","L"))
		Call WriteCel(Sh,i+4, 9, 12, 11.14,	"Arial", 9, True, True, xlLeft,"@",			AlanUzunluklari(miktar ,	tbl_EBEYNM21,i,tbl_EBEYNM2A,"FT_MAL_V_HIZMETMIKTR",	"CFLAG8","L"))
		Call WriteCel(Sh,i+4,10, 12, 15,		"Arial", 9, True, True, xlRight,"#,##0.00",	AlanUzunluklari(KDVharic ,	tbl_EBEYNM21,i,tbl_EBEYNM2A,"FT_IVD_KH_TOPLAM",		"CFLAG9","R"))
		Call WriteCel(Sh,i+4,11, 12, 15,		"Arial", 9, True, True, xlRight,"#,##0.00",	AlanUzunluklari(KDV,		tbl_EBEYNM21,i,tbl_EBEYNM2A,"FT_KDV_TUTARI",		"CFLAG10","R"))
		Call WriteCel(Sh,i+4,12, 12, 15,		"Arial", 9, True, True, xlRight,"#,##0.00",	AlanUzunluklari(tevkifat ,	tbl_EBEYNM21,i,tbl_EBEYNM2A,"FT_TARIHI",			"CFLAG11","R"))

		KDVharic_TOPLAM=KDVharic_TOPLAM+KDVharic 
		KDV_TUTARI=KDV_TUTARI+KDVharic
		Tevkifat_TOPLAM=Tevkifat_TOPLAM+tevkifat
	next

	tbl_EBEYNM21.SetCurrentRow 1

	FT_IVD_KH_TOPLAM=Doc.dRound(KDVharic_TOPLAM)
	FT_KDV_TUTARI=Doc.dRound(KDV_TUTARI)

	Dim Sum1
	Dim Sum2
	Dim Sum3

	Sum1="=SUM(J5:J@)"
	Sum2="=SUM(K5:K@)"
	Sum3="=SUM(L5:L@)"

	Sum1=RePlace(Sum1,"J@","J"&i+4-1)
	Sum2=RePlace(Sum2,"K@","K"&i+4-1)
	Sum3=RePlace(Sum3,"L@","L"&i+4-1)

'	Call WriteCel(Sh,i+4,14, 12, 16.14,	"Arial", 9, True, True, xlLeft,"=SUM()")
	Call WriteCel(Sh,i+4,  9, 12, 11.14,"Arial", 9, True, True, xlLeft,"","TOPLAM")
'	Call WriteCel(Sh,i+4, 10, 12, 15,	"Arial", 9, True, True, xlRight,"#,##0.00",FT_IVD_KH_TOPLAM)
'	Call WriteCel(Sh,i+4, 11, 12, 15,	"Arial", 9, True, True, xlRight,"#,##0.00",FT_KDV_TUTARI)
	Call WriteCel(Sh,i+4, 10, 12, 15,	"Arial", 9, True, True, xlRight,"#,##0.00",Sum1)
	Call WriteCel(Sh,i+4, 11, 12, 15,	"Arial", 9, True, True, xlRight,"#,##0.00",Sum2)
	Call WriteCel(Sh,i+4, 12, 12, 15,	"Arial", 9, True, True, xlRight,"#,##0.00",Sum3)

End Sub


Function CreateIfNotExistDir(vDr)
	Dim fs
	Dim newfolder
	CreateIfNotExistDir=False
	Set fs=CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
	if Not fs.FolderExists(vDr) then
		Set newfolder=fs.CreateFolder(vDr) 
		if Not IsObject(newfolder) then
			CreateIfNotExistDir=TRUE
			Doc.d7MsgBox vDr + " Dizin Oluþturulamiyor.","Freedom",MB_OK
			Exit Function
		end if
	end if
	set fs=nothing	
End Function

Sub WriteCel(ObjSheet,RowNum, ColNum, pRowHeight, pColumnWidth, FontName, FontSize, FontBold, pWrapText,pHorizontalAlignment,pNumberFormat,pAciklama)
    With ObjSheet.Cells(RowNum, ColNum)
		  if Trim(pNumberFormat)<>"" then
			  .NumberFormat = pNumberFormat
		  end if
'        .FormulaR1C1 = pAciklama
			.Formula = pAciklama
'        .RowHeight = pRowHeight '76.75
        .HorizontalAlignment = pHorizontalAlignment 'xlCenter
        .VerticalAlignment = xlCenter
        .WrapText = pWrapText 'True
        .Orientation = 0
        .AddIndent = False
        .IndentLevel = 0
        .ShrinkToFit = False
        .ReadingOrder = xlContext
        .MergeCells = False
        .Font.Name = FontName '"Arial"
        .Font.Size = FontSize '9
        .Font.Bold = FontBold 'True
        .ColumnWidth = pColumnWidth '3.71
    End With
    With ObjSheet.Cells(RowNum, ColNum).Borders(xlEdgeLeft)
        .LineStyle = xlContinuous
        .Weight = xlThin
        .ColorIndex = xlAutomatic
    End With
    With ObjSheet.Cells(RowNum, ColNum).Borders(xlEdgeTop)
        .LineStyle = xlContinuous
        .Weight = xlThin
        .ColorIndex = xlAutomatic
    End With
    With ObjSheet.Cells(RowNum, ColNum).Borders(xlEdgeBottom)
        .LineStyle = xlContinuous
        .Weight = xlThin
        .ColorIndex = xlAutomatic
    End With
		With ObjSheet.Cells(RowNum, ColNum).Borders(xlEdgeRight)
			.LineStyle = xlContinuous
			.Weight = xlThin
			.ColorIndex = xlAutomatic
    End With

End Sub


Function AlanUzunluklari(XmlValue,tbl_T,i,tbl_L,LenFieldName,FlagFieldName,JusTify)
	Dim FieldLen
	Dim Fieldflag
	AlanUzunluklari="" & XmlValue
	AlanUzunluklari=Trim(AlanUzunluklari)
	AlanUzunluklari=RePlace(AlanUzunluklari,Space(2),Space(1))
	AlanUzunluklari=RePlace(AlanUzunluklari,Space(2),Space(1))

	if Len(AlanUzunluklari)<=0 then Exit Function

	FieldLen=tbl_L.GetFieldValue(LenFieldName,-1)
	Fieldflag=tbl_L.GetFieldValue(FlagFieldName,-1)

	if FieldLen=0 then Exit Function
	if Trim(Fieldflag)="1" then
		if JusTify="R" then
			AlanUzunluklari=Right(AlanUzunluklari,FieldLen)
		elseif JusTify="L" then
			AlanUzunluklari=Left(AlanUzunluklari,FieldLen)
		end if
		Exit Function
	end if

	if Len(AlanUzunluklari)>FieldLen then
		Doc.d7MsgBox i & ". Satir. Belirtilen Alan("+LenFieldName+") Uzunluðu=" & FieldLen & " Yazilmaya Çaliþilan Bilginin Uzunluðu=" & Len(AlanUzunluklari),"Freedom",MB_OK
		AlanUzunluklari=" "
	end if
End Function


Sub DeleteFile(FileName)
	dim fs
	Set fs=CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
	if fs.FileExists(FileName) then
	  fs.DeleteFile(FileName)
	end if
	set fs=nothing
End Sub

Sub Makro1()
	Set MTable=Doc.CreateTableObject("MAKROTABLE=STACK","IO")
	MTable.Addfield2 "RAPORPB","CHAR",4,0,"L","","*PUNIT"
	' MTable.Addfield2 "E_TARIH","DATE",10,0,"L","",""
	
	Set MTableT=Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("MAKROTABLET=STACK","IO")
	MTableT.Addfield2 "OP_NO","CHAR",10,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "KOD","CHAR",24,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('STOK00')",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "AD","CHAR",50,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW(""STOK00"",""KOD=KOD"",""AD"",""BRW_STOK00_AD"")",""
	
	MTableT.SetMacroModuleTableName "MAKROTABLET"
	MTableT.Setd7FieldEvent "KOD","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "KOD_OV"
	
	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing
	Doc.LoadSubForm_Dyn "MAKROFORMU1",SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME,Par
	
	MTable.RAPORPB = "EURO"
	MTableT.SetRowAID 1,1,1
End Sub
Sub MiktarSureHesapla()
	if OpNoGirilmemis Then Exit Sub
	Call Hesapla("MIKTAR_SURE")
End Sub
Sub MaliyetHesapla()
	Set gg = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")
	if OpNoGirilmemis Then Exit Sub
	Call Hesapla("MALIYET")
End Sub
Sub Hesapla(HesapOp)
	Set RepTable = GetRaporTable()
	Set MTableE = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET")
	
	Call DefaultBasliklariEkle(RepTable)

	RaporPB = MTableE.RAPORPB
	For i = 1 to MTableT.GetRowCount()
		if trim(MTableT.KOD(i)) <> "" Then
			Call AtolyeKayitlariniOku(MTableT.KOD(i), MTableT.OP_NO(i), RepTable, RaporPB)
			if HesapOp = "MALIYET" Then Call ModelMasraflariniOku(MTableT.KOD(i), MTableT.OP_NO(i), RepTable, RaporPB)
			Call HammaddeleriOku(MTableT.KOD(i), MTableT.OP_NO(i), RepTable, RaporPB)
			if HesapOp = "MALIYET" Then Call FasonMasraflariniOku(MTableT.KOD(i), MTableT.OP_NO(i), RepTable, RaporPB)
			if HesapOp = "MALIYET" Then Call MuhasebeMasraflariOku(MTableT.KOD(i), MTableT.OP_NO(i), RepTable, RaporPB)
		End if
	Next

	Set Excel = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
	Set Wb1 = Excel.Workbooks.Add
	
	RepTable.Sort("BOMREC_SIRANO+OP_NO+RAPOR_GRUBU+RAPOR_ALT_GRUBU")
	Call DetaylariYazdir(Excel, Wb1, RepTable)

	RepTable.GroupBy("BOMREC_SIRANO+OP_NO+RAPOR_GRUBU+RAPOR_ALT_GRUBU")
	Call RaporYazdir(Excel, Wb1, RepTable, HesapOp)
	'Call SendTableToExcel(RepTable)
End Sub
Function GetRaporTable()
	Doc.CreateTableObject "xxx___REP=STACK", "TMPCOPY"
	Set GetRaporTable = Doc.GetTableObject("xxx___REP")
	GetRaporTable.AddField2 "EVRAKNO","CHAR",10,0,"R","",""
	GetRaporTable.AddField2 "OP_NO","CHAR",10,0,"L","",""
	GetRaporTable.AddField2 "BOMREC_SIRANO","CHAR",10,0,"L","",""
	GetRaporTable.AddField2 "BOMREC_ALT_SIRANO","CHAR",10,0,"L","",""
	GetRaporTable.AddField2 "BOMREC_CODE","CHAR",24,0,"L","",""
	GetRaporTable.AddField2 "BOMREC_INPUTTYPE","CHAR",1,0,"L","",""
	GetRaporTable.AddField2 "BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE","CHAR",24,0,"L","",""
	GetRaporTable.AddField2 "BOMREC_KAYNAK0","DOUBLE",24,6,"R","",""
	GetRaporTable.AddField2 "BOMREC_KAYNAK1","DOUBLE",24,6,"R","",""
	GetRaporTable.AddField2 "SF_UNIT","CHAR",6,0,"L","",""
	GetRaporTable.AddField2 "RAPOR_GRUBU","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	GetRaporTable.AddField2 "RAPOR_ALT_GRUBU","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	GetRaporTable.AddField2 "OR_FIYAT","DOUBLE",20,4,"R","",""
	GetRaporTable.AddField2 "PRICEUNIT","CHAR",4,0,"L","",""
	GetRaporTable.AddField2 "OR_TUTAR","DOUBLE",20,2,"R","",""
	GetRaporTable.AddField2 "RPB_TUTAR","DOUBLE",20,2,"R","",""
	GetRaporTable.AddField2 "RPB_OPTUTAR","DOUBLE",20,2,"R","",""
	GetRaporTable.AddField2 "SURE_HARIC","CHAR",1,0,"L","",""
End Function
Sub AtolyeKayitlariniOku(StokKodu, OpNo, RepTable, RaporPB)

	' Set g = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")
	Set S20T = Doc.Select1G("dw4r5gggg", "SFDC20T", "MAMULKOD>='" & trim(StokKodu) & "'&MAMULKOD<='" & trim(StokKodu) & "zzzzz'", "","EVRAKTYPE+EVRAKNO+JOBNO+RECTARIH1+RECTIME1",-1)
	Call PresAltiCalismasiIcinTezgahSureleriniHesapla(S20T)

	For i = 1 to S20T.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Atölye Kayýtlarý Okunuyor : " & i & " / " & S20T.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = RepTable.AddRow()
		GK4 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("IMLT00", "GK_4", S20T.KOD(i), "")
		GK5 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("IMLT00", "GK_5", S20T.KOD(i), "")
		if trim(S20T.KOD(i)) = "MUH" Then
			RepTable.BOMREC_SIRANO(satirNo) = "01"
			RepTable.RAPOR_GRUBU(satirNo) = "CAD/CAM/PROJE TAKIP"
			RepTable.RAPOR_ALT_GRUBU(satirNo) = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("IMLT01", "AD", S20T.OPERASYON(i), "")
			MesaiVar = False
		Elseif trim(S20T.OPERASYON(i)) = "MÜÞTES" Then
			RepTable.BOMREC_SIRANO(satirNo) = "09"
			RepTable.RAPOR_GRUBU(satirNo) = "MÜÞTERÝ PRESÝNDE TESLÝM"
			RepTable.RAPOR_ALT_GRUBU(satirNo) = "ÝÞÇÝLÝK"
			MesaiVar = False
		Elseif trim(GK4) = "TAL" Then
			RepTable.BOMREC_SIRANO(satirNo) = "05"
			RepTable.BOMREC_ALT_SIRANO(satirNo) = trim(Doc.GetGecoValue("I00GK5", "AD_L2", GK5, -1))
			RepTable.RAPOR_GRUBU(satirNo) = "TALAÞLI ÝMALAT"
			RepTable.RAPOR_ALT_GRUBU(satirNo) = trim(Doc.GetGecoValue("I00GK5", "AD", GK5, -1))

			MesaiVar = True
			TalasliImalat = True
		Elseif trim(GK4) = "KALIP DENE" Then
			RepTable.BOMREC_SIRANO(satirNo) = "06"
			RepTable.RAPOR_GRUBU(satirNo) = "TESVÝYE/MONTAJ/DENEME"
			RepTable.RAPOR_ALT_GRUBU(satirNo) = "PRES ALTI ÇALIÞMASI"
			MesaiVar = True
			TalasliImalat = False
			PresAltiCalismasi = True
		Else
			RepTable.BOMREC_SIRANO(satirNo) = "06"
			RepTable.RAPOR_GRUBU(satirNo) = "TESVÝYE/MONTAJ/DENEME"
			RepTable.RAPOR_ALT_GRUBU(satirNo) = "TESVÝYE MONTAJ"
			MesaiVar = True
			TalasliImalat = False
		End if
		if Not MesaiVar Then
			MesaiSuresi = 0
		Else
			MesaiArr = GetMesaiSure(S20T.RECTARIH1(i), S20T.RECTIME1(i), S20T.RECTARIH2(i), S20T.RECTIME2(i), TalasliImalat)
			MesaiSuresi = MesaiArr(0) + MesaiArr(1)
		End if
		RepTable.OP_NO(satirNo) = OpNo
		RepTable.BOMREC_CODE(satirNo) = StokKodu
		RepTable.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE(satirNo) = "I"
		RepTable.EVRAKNO(satirNo) = S20T.EVRAKNO(i)
		RepTable.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(satirNo) = S20T.KOD(i)
		if PresAltiCalismasi Then
			if S20T.SURE(i) > 0 Then
				RepTable.BOMREC_KAYNAK0(satirNo) = S20T.SURE(i)' - MesaiSuresi
			Else
				RepTable.BOMREC_KAYNAK0(satirNo) = S20T.SURE(i)
			End if
			RepTable.BOMREC_KAYNAK1(satirNo) = S20T.TOPLAM_OPRTR_S_ST(i)
		Else
			RepTable.BOMREC_KAYNAK0(satirNo) = S20T.SURE(i) '- MesaiSuresi
			RepTable.BOMREC_KAYNAK1(satirNo) = S20T.TOPLAM_OPRTR_S_ST(i) '- MesaiSuresi
		End if
		RepTable.SF_UNIT(satirNo) = "SAAT"
'		Fiyat = Doc.StandartMaliyet_K("I", S20T.KOD(i),1,"SAAT", Doc.BuGun,"YL","YL", RaporPB)
		ToplamTezgahMaliyeti = GetToplamOperasyonMaliyeti(S20T.KOD(i), RepTable.BOMREC_KAYNAK0(satirNo), RepTable.BOMREC_KAYNAK1(satirNo), Doc.BuGun, "YL", RaporPB)
		RepTable.OR_FIYAT(satirNo) = 0
		RepTable.PRICEUNIT(satirNo) = RaporPB
		RepTable.OR_TUTAR(satirNo) = ToplamTezgahMaliyeti
		RepTable.RPB_TUTAR(satirNo) = ToplamTezgahMaliyeti
		RepTable.RPB_OPTUTAR(satirNo) = ToplamTezgahMaliyeti
		if MesaiSuresi > 0 and (not PresAltiCalismasi or (PresAltiCalismasi and S20T.TOPLAM_OPRTR_S_ST(i) = 0)) Then
			satirNo = RepTable.AddRow()
			if trim(GK4) = "TAL" Then
				RepTable.BOMREC_SIRANO(satirNo) = "05"
				RepTable.RAPOR_GRUBU(satirNo) = "TALAÞLI ÝMALAT"
				GrupAdi = Doc.GetGecoValue("I00GK5", "AD", GK5, -1)
				RepTable.RAPOR_ALT_GRUBU(satirNo) = trim(GrupAdi) & " FAZLA MESAÝ EK MALÝYETÝ"
				RepTable.BOMREC_ALT_SIRANO(satirNo) = trim(Doc.GetGecoValue("I00GK5", "AD_L2", GK5, -1))
				MesaiVar = True
				RepTable.SURE_HARIC(satirNo) = "E"
			Elseif trim(GK4) = "KALIP DENE" Then
				RepTable.BOMREC_SIRANO(satirNo) = "06"
				RepTable.RAPOR_GRUBU(satirNo) = "TESVÝYE/MONTAJ/DENEME"
				RepTable.RAPOR_ALT_GRUBU(satirNo) = "PRES ALTI ÇALIÞMASI FAZLA MESAÝ EK MALÝYETÝ"
				MesaiVar = True
				RepTable.SURE_HARIC(satirNo) = "E"
			Else
				RepTable.BOMREC_SIRANO(satirNo) = "06"
				RepTable.RAPOR_GRUBU(satirNo) = "TESVÝYE/MONTAJ/DENEME"
				RepTable.RAPOR_ALT_GRUBU(satirNo) = "TESVÝYE MONTAJ FAZLA MESAÝ EK MALÝYETÝ"
				MesaiVar = True
				RepTable.SURE_HARIC(satirNo) = "E"
			End if
			
			RepTable.OP_NO(satirNo) = OpNo
			RepTable.BOMREC_CODE(satirNo) = StokKodu
			RepTable.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE(satirNo) = "I"
			RepTable.EVRAKNO(satirNo) = S20T.EVRAKNO(i)
			RepTable.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(satirNo) = S20T.KOD(i)
			RepTable.BOMREC_KAYNAK0(satirNo) = MesaiArr(0) + MesaiArr(1)
			RepTable.BOMREC_KAYNAK1(satirNo) = MesaiArr(0) + MesaiArr(1)
			RepTable.SF_UNIT(satirNo) = "SAAT"
			IscilikFiyati = GetUnsurOpMl(S20T.KOD(i), "YL", "DÝ", Doc.BuGun, RaporPB)
			RepTable.OR_FIYAT(satirNo) = MesaiArr(0) * IscilikFiyati * 0.5 + MesaiArr(1) * IscilikFiyati
			RepTable.PRICEUNIT(satirNo) = RaporPB
			RepTable.OR_TUTAR(satirNo) = MesaiArr(0) * IscilikFiyati * 0.5 + MesaiArr(1) * IscilikFiyati
			RepTable.RPB_TUTAR(satirNo) = MesaiArr(0) * IscilikFiyati * 0.5 + MesaiArr(1) * IscilikFiyati
			RepTable.RPB_OPTUTAR(satirNo) = MesaiArr(0) * IscilikFiyati * 0.5 + MesaiArr(1) * IscilikFiyati
		End if
	Next
End Sub
Sub SendTableToExcel(Tablo)
	Set pe2 = Doc.CreateSObject("ExcelOutput")
	PE2.SetOrientation "P"
	PE2.PageB_ReportHead_std1 Tablo.GetAppName
	
	Alanlar = Tablo.GetFields()
	ncol = 0
	For i=0 To uBound(Alanlar,1)
		AlanAdi = Alanlar(i,0)
		if Cikacak(AlanAdi) Then
			ncol = ncol+1
			if ncol > 256 Then Exit For
			AlanTuru = Alanlar(i,2)
			If AlanTuru = "INT16" or AlanTuru = "INT32" or AlanTuru = "DOUBLE" Then
				Align = "RIGHT"
			Else
				Align = "LEFT"
			End if
			pe2.AddColumnDef Alanlar(i,0), Alanlar(i,3), Alanlar(i,4), Align
			pe2.SetRowValue_String -1, AlanAdi
		End if
	Next
	PE2.LineOut
	
	For satirNo = 1 to Tablo.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Excel 'e gönderiliyor : " & Tablo.GetAppName & " - " & i & " / " & Tablo.GetRowCount
		PE2.InitRowValues
		
		ExcelSatir = -1
		ncol = 0
		For i=0 To uBound(Alanlar,1)
			AlanAdi = Alanlar(i,0)
			if Cikacak(AlanAdi) Then
				ncol = ncol+1
				if ncol > 256 Then Exit For
				AlanTuru = Alanlar(i,2)
				If AlanTuru = "INT16" or AlanTuru = "INT32" or AlanTuru = "DOUBLE" Then
					Deger = Tablo.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi,satirNo)
					pe2.SetRowValue_Double ExcelSatir, Deger
				ElseIf AlanTuru = "DATE" Then
					OutputStr=Doc.StrToNorDate(Tablo.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi,satirNo))
					pe2.SetRowValue_String ExcelSatir,OutputStr
				Else
					pe2.SetRowValue_String ExcelSatir,Tablo.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi,satirNo)
				End if
			End if
		Next
		PE2.LineOut
	Next
	pe2.Show
End Sub
Function Cikacak(AlanAdi)
	Cikacak = True
	if InStr(1, AlanAdi, "REPORT__") > 0 Then
		Cikacak = False
	End if
	if InStr(1, AlanAdi, "BOMREC_CODE_") > 0 Then
		Cikacak = False
	End if
End Function
Sub HammaddeleriOku(StokKodu, OpNo, RepTable, RaporPB)
	QStr = "SF_VRI_VR_R1>='" & trim(StokKodu) & "'&SF_VRI_VR_R1<='" & trim(StokKodu) & "zzzz'"
	Set S29T = Doc.Select1("d4f44gg", "STOK29T", QStr, "", -1)
'	Call FiyatiOlmayanIrsaliyelereSiparistenFiyatGetir(Doc, S29T)
	
	OncekiFirma = Doc.Customp_Gets("_DV_OFR")
	Set Doc_Old = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI2(Doc.GetDocType, "", OncekiFirma)
	Set S29T_Old = Doc_Old.Select1("dX4f44gg", "STOK29T", QStr, "", -1)
'	Call FiyatiOlmayanIrsaliyelereSiparistenFiyatGetir(S29T_Old, S29T_Old)
	if S29T_Old.GetRowCount() > 0 Then S29T.AppendTable(S29T_Old)
	
	For i = 1 to S29T.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Satýnalma Kayýtlarý Okunuyor : " & i & " / " & S29T.GetRowCount()
		if Left(S29T.KOD(i), 8) <> "3050 K60" Then
			satirNo = RepTable.AddRow()
			GK8 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "GK_8", S29T.KOD(i), "")
			if Left(S29T.KOD(i), 7) = "3050 K0" Then
				RepTable.BOMREC_SIRANO(satirNo) = "03"
				RepTable.RAPOR_GRUBU(satirNo) = "DÖKÜM"
				RepTable.RAPOR_ALT_GRUBU(satirNo) = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", S29T.KOD(i), "")
			Elseif trim(GK8) = "KALIP TAS" Then
				RepTable.BOMREC_SIRANO(satirNo) = "01"
				RepTable.RAPOR_GRUBU(satirNo) = "CAD/CAM/PROJE TAKIP"
				RepTable.RAPOR_ALT_GRUBU(satirNo) = Doc.GetGecoValue("STKGK8", "AD", GK8, -1)
			Else
				RepTable.BOMREC_SIRANO(satirNo) = "04"
				RepTable.RAPOR_GRUBU(satirNo) = "STANDART MALZEME"
				AnaKod = Doc.Kod_SeviyeAl(S29T.KOD(i), -1)
				RepTable.RAPOR_ALT_GRUBU(satirNo) = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", AnaKod, "")
			End if
			RepTable.OP_NO(satirNo) = OpNo
			RepTable.BOMREC_CODE(satirNo) = StokKodu
			RepTable.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE(satirNo) = "H"
			RepTable.EVRAKNO(satirNo) = S29T.EVRAKNO(i)
			RepTable.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(satirNo) = S29T.KOD(i)
			RepTable.BOMREC_KAYNAK0(satirNo) = S29T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
			RepTable.SF_UNIT(satirNo) = S29T.SF_SF_UNIT(i)
			RepTable.OR_FIYAT(satirNo) = S29T.OR_FIYAT(i)
			RepTable.PRICEUNIT(satirNo) = S29T.PUNIT_ORG(i)
			RepTable.OR_TUTAR(satirNo) = S29T.OR_TUTAR(i)
			Kur = Doc.GetKurN(S29T.PUNIT_ORG(i), RaporPB, Doc.BuGun, "", -1)
			RepTable.RPB_TUTAR(satirNo) = S29T.OR_TUTAR(i) * Kur
			RepTable.RPB_OPTUTAR(satirNo) = 0
		End if
	Next
End Sub

		satirNo = RepTable.AddRow()
		RepTable.OP_NO(satirNo) = OpNo
		RepTable.BOMREC_CODE(satirNo) = StokKodu

Sub Makro1()
	DocYaratildi = False
	For i = 1 to LISTET.GetRowCount()
		if trim(LISTET.KOD(i)) <> "" Then
			Set B01E1 = Doc.Select1("wdf234fsdf", "BOMU01E", "AKTIF_PASIF='*'&MAMULCODE='" & LISTET.KOD(i) & "'", "", -1)
			if B01E1.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
				if not DocYaratildi Then
					Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("BOMU01", "")
					Set B01E = Doc1.GetTableObject("BOMU01E")
					Set B01T = Doc1.GetTableObject("BOMU01T")
					DocYaratildi = True
				End if
				Doc1.New_Voucher
				B01E.MAMULCODE = LISTET.KOD(i)
				B01E.MAMULMIKTAR = 1

				satirNo = B01T.AddRow()
				B01T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
				B01T.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE = "I"
				B01T.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE = "KGM"
				B01T.BOMREC_OPERASYON = "MONTAJ"

				satirNo = B01T.AddRow()
				B01T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
				B01T.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE = "KGM"
				B01T.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE = "PRESAL"
				
				satirNo = B01T.AddRow()
				B01T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
				B01T.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE = "KGM"
				B01T.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE = "MÜÞTES"
	
				satirNo = B01T.AddRow()
				B01T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
				B01T.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE = "I"
				B01T.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE = "KGT"
				B01T.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE = "TAL"
				
				satirNo = B01T.AddRow()
				B01T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
				B01T.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE = "I"
				B01T.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE = "MUH"
				B01T.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE = "CAD"
				
				satirNo = B01T.AddRow()
				B01T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
				B01T.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE = "I"
				B01T.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE = "MUH"
				B01T.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE = "CAM"
				
				satirNo = B01T.AddRow()
				B01T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
				B01T.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE = "I"
				B01T.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE = "MUH"
				B01T.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE = "PROJE"

					Doc1.Save_Voucher
				Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
			End if
		End if
	Next
End Sub

Sub StokKoduGecersizIseUyariVer(Tablo, VaryantField)
	Varyant = Tablo.GetFieldValue(VaryantField, -1)
	if trim(Varyant) = "" Then Exit Sub
	Set S00 = Doc.Select1("w23fdwdf", "STOK00", "KOD='" & Varyant & "'", "", -1)
	if S00.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox trim(Varyant) & " stok kartý bulunamadý.", "Hata", MB_OK
		Tablo.SetFieldValue VaryantField, -1, ""
	Else
		if S00.CODETYPE = "A" Then
			Doc.d7MsgBox trim(Varyant) & " kodu detay deðil. Yalnýzca Detay kodlardan seçim yapýlabilir", "Hata", MB_OK
			Tablo.SetFieldValue VaryantField, -1, ""
		End if
	End if
End Sub
Sub KapaliMPSIseUyariVer(Tablo, VaryantField)
	Varyant = Tablo.GetFieldValue(VaryantField, -1)
	Set M10E = Doc.Select1("fdsef34bfff", "MMPS10E", "MAMULSTOKKODU='" & Varyant & "'&HAVUZKODU='KL'", "", -1)
	if M10E.GetRowCount() <> 1 Then Exit Sub
	if M10E.ACIK_KAPALI="K" Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Seçilen projeye ait " & trim(M10E.EVRAKNO) & " no.lu MPS kapatýlmýþ", "Uyarý", MB_OK
		Tablo.SetFieldValue VaryantField, -1, ""
	End if
End Sub
Function ZZKodunaVaryantGirilmis(Tablo, VaryantField)
	ZZKodunaVaryantGirilmis = False
	Set ff = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")
	Set Tbl1 = Doc.Select2("asr34hsadh", Tablo, "KOD>='ZZ'&KOD<='ZZZZZZZ'&" & VaryantField & ">='1050 '&" & VaryantField & "<='1050 zzzzz'", "", -1)
	if Tbl1.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "ZZ ile baþlayan kodlarýn Varyant-1 'ine 1050 ile baþlayan kod girilemez. Lütfen 4050 K90 00640 kodunu kullanýn", "", MB_OK
		ZZKodunaVaryantGirilmis = False
	End if
End Function

Sub Makro1()
	QStr = "SELECT STOK74T.EVRAKNO FROM STOK74T"
	QStr = QStr & " INNER JOIN STOK74T1 ON STOK74T1.EVRAKNO=STOK74T.EVRAKNO AND STOK74T1.OR_TRNUM=STOK74T.TRNUM"
	QStr = QStr & " INNER JOIN STOK68T ON STOK74T1.FI_SYSIRSALIYEPOSNO=STOK68T.SYSIRSALIYEPOSNO"
	QStr = QStr & " WHERE STOK68T.TL_FIYAT=0 AND STOK74T.OR_FIYAT>0"
	QStr = QStr & " GROUP BY STOK74T.EVRAKNO"
	Set S74T = Doc.RunSQLQuery("dsagwegs", QStr)
	For i = 1 to S74T.GetRowCount()
		Doc.Load_Voucher S74T.EVRAKNO(i)
		Doc.Save_Voucher
	Next
End Sub

Sub Makro1()
	Set ESL_ET = Doc.GetTableObject("ESL_LIST_XXX")
	if Doc.IsBlank(ESL_ET) Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Eþleþtirme kaydý bulunamadý", "", MB_OK

	End if

		Doc.Delete_Voucher
	Next

Glo_path="F:\PUANTAJ\PUANTAJ.XLS"

Glo_satirsayisi = 350

Sub Makro1()
'	on error resume next

	dim strFpath,intSatirSayisi
	Fpath = Glo_path
	strFpath = inputbox("Excel dosyasýnýn yolunu giriniz","Puantaj dosyasýnýn yolu",Glo_path)

	if trim(strFpath)="" Then 
		msgbox("Boþ dosya adý olamaz.Makroyu tekrar çalýþtýrýn.")
		exit Sub
	End if

	intSatirSayisi = inputbox("Excel dosyasýndan okunacak satýr sayýsý","Excel'deki satýr sayýsý",Glo_satirsayisi)

	if not isNumeric(intSatirSayisi) Then 
		msgbox("Boþ ya da yanlýþ satýr bildirimi.Makroyu tekrar çalýþtýrýn.")
		exit Sub
	End if

	dim objExcel
	Set objExcel = CreateObject("Excel.Application")

	dim objWorkbooks
	Set objWorkbooks = objExcel.Workbooks

	objWorkbooks.Open(strFpath)
	
	dim objSheet1, sutun
	Set objSheet1 = objWorkbooks(1).Worksheets(1)
	dim c,strPersNo
	dim dblNormalCalisma,dblHaftatatili,dblGenelTatil,dblYillikizin,dblucretliizin,dblGenelTatil1
	dim dblIstirahat,dblucretsiz,dbl100mesai,dbl50mesai,dbldevamsiz,dbleksik,dblmazaret1,dblmazaret2
	dim dblIstirahat2, dblIstirahat1
	if err.number<>0 Then 
		objWorkbooks(1).Close
		objExcel.Quit
		Set objExcel=Nothing
		Set objWorkbooks=Nothing
		Set objSheet1=Nothing
		Exit Sub
	End if	

	For c=1 to BRDR1PBGIRIS.GetRecCount
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Puantaj Bilgileri Aktarýlýyor : " & c & " / " & BRDR1PBGIRIS.GetRecCount

		SicilNo = BRDR1PBGIRIS.C_KOD(c)
		PdksNo = doc.getdbfieldvalue("PERSM0","KOD",SicilNo,"")
		strPersNo = trim(PdksNo)

		BRDR1PBGIRIS.SetCurrentRow(c)

		if TRUE Then
		'if trim(BRDR1PBGIRIS.C_KOD(c)) = "2016" Then
			sutun=1
			For ik=2 to Glo_satirsayisi
				if Trim(objSheet1.Cells(ik,sutun))=trim(strPersNo) Then
					satir = ik
					Exit For
				End if
			Next

			strPersNoExcel = objSheet1.Cells(satir,sutun).Value
			i = satir
	
			if trim(strPersNoExcel) <> "" Then
	
				if Trim(strPersNoExcel)=Trim(strPersNo) Then

           'NORMAL ÇALIÞMA1 (gün)
					if isnumeric(objSheet1.Cells(i,3).Value) Then
						dblNormalCalisma1=xCdbl(objSheet1.Cells(i,3).Value)
					Else
						dblNormalCalisma1=xCdbl(0)
					End if
				  	'dblNormalCalisma1=Doc.div(dblNormalCalisma,7.5)
					'dblNormalCalisma1=Doc.dround(dblNormalCalisma1)
					'dblNormalCalisma1 = Doc.Round(dblNormalCalisma1,0)
             
            'HAFTA TATÝLÝ1 (gün)
					if isnumeric(objSheet1.Cells(i,11).Value) Then
						dblHaftatatili1=xCdbl(objSheet1.Cells(i,11).Value)
					Else
						dblhaftatatili1=xCdbl(0)
					End if
            					
					'GENEL TATÝL (SAAT)
				'	if isnumeric(objSheet1.Cells(i,14).Value) Then
				'		dblGenelTatil=xCdbl(objSheet1.Cells(i,14).Value)
				'	Else
				'		dblGenelTatil=xCdbl(0)
				'	End if
					'GENEL TATÝL (GÜN)
					if isnumeric(objSheet1.Cells(i,14).Value) Then
						dblGenelTatil1=xCdbl(objSheet1.Cells(i,14).Value)
					Else
						dblGenelTatil1=xCdbl(0)
					End if

				'	'YILLIK IZIN1 (GÜN)
				'	if isnumeric(objSheet1.Cells(i,16).Value) Then
				'		dblYillikizin1=xCdbl(objSheet1.Cells(i,16).Value)
				'	Else
				'		dblYillikizin1=xCdbl(0)
				'	End if
					'ÜCRETLÝ ÝZÝN (GÜN)
					if isnumeric(objSheet1.Cells(i,13).Value) Then
						dblucretliizin1=xCdbl(objSheet1.Cells(i,13).Value)
					Else
						dblucretliizin1=xCdbl(0)
					End if
				'	dblucretliizin1=Doc.div(dblucretliizin,7.5)
				'	dblucretliizin1=Doc.dround(dblucretliizin1)
           
			   'DEVAMSIZ(GÜN)
					if isnumeric(objSheet1.Cells(i,5).Value) Then
						dbldevamsiz1=xCdbl(objSheet1.Cells(i,5).Value)
					Else
						dbldevamsiz1=xCdbl(0)
					End if
				'	dbldevamsiz1=Doc.div(dbldevamsiz,7.5)
				'	dbldevamsiz1=Doc.dround(dbldevamsiz1)
        	
					'ÜCRETSÝZ ÝZÝN(SAAT)
					if isnumeric(objSheet1.Cells(i,4).Value) Then
						dblucretsiz = xCdbl(objSheet1.Cells(i,4).Value)
					Else
						dblucretsiz = xCdbl(0)
					End if
					'ÜCRETSÝZ ÝZÝN (GÜN)
					if isnumeric(objSheet1.Cells(i,5).Value) Then
						dblucretsiz1 = xCdbl(objSheet1.Cells(i,5).Value)
					Else
						dblucretsiz1 = xCdbl(0)
					End if
				'	dblucretsiz1=Doc.div(dblucretsiz,7.5)
				'	dblucretsiz1=Doc.dround(dblucretsiz1)
                      
  	          'ISTIRAHAT1(GÜN)
					if isnumeric(objSheet1.Cells(i,24).Value) Then
						dblIstirahat1 = xCdbl(objSheet1.Cells(i,24).Value)
					Else
						dblIstirahat1 = xCdbl(0)
					End if
                  
				'	'%50 MESAI (SAAT)
				'	if isnumeric(objSheet1.Cells(i,6).Value) Then
				'		dbl50mesai=xCdbl(objSheet1.Cells(i,6).Value)
				'	Else
				'		dbl50mesai=xCdbl(0)
				'	End if
				'	'%100 MESAI(SAAT)
				'	if isnumeric(objSheet1.Cells(i,8).Value) Then 
				'		dbl100mesai=xCdbl(objSheet1.Cells(i,8).Value)
				'	Else
				'		dbl100mesai=xCdbl(0)
				'	End if


' -------------------------------------------------
				'	SskToplami = 0
				'	SskToplami = BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCG01(c)+BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCG02(c)+BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCG03(c)+BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCG04(c)+BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCG05(c)+BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KIG01(c)+BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KIG02(c)+BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KIG06(c)+BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KIG07(c)
				'	If SskToplami>=31 then
				'		BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCG02(c) = BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCG02(c) - 1
				'	end if
' -------------------------------------------------

				'	BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AS_KCS01(c) = dblNormalCalisma
					BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AG_KCG01(c) = dblNormalCalisma1
           ' BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AS_KCS02(c) = dblHaftatatili
					BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AG_KCG02(c) = dblHaftatatili1
          '  BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AS_KCS03(c) = dblGenelTatil
					BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AG_KCG03(c) = dblGenelTatil1
				'	BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AS_KCS04(c) = dblYillikizin
				'	BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AG_KCG04(c) = dblYillikizin1
          '  BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AS_KCS05(c) = dblucretliizin
           BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AG_KCG05(c) = dblucretliizin1
			'		BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AS_KIG01(c) = dblIstirahat2
				 	BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AG_KIG01(c) = dblIstirahat1
          '  BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AS_KIG02(c) = dblucretsiz+dbldevamsiz
            BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AG_KIG05(c) = dbldevamsiz1

				'	BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_E_AS_E01(c) = dbl50mesai
				'	BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_E_AS_E02(c) = dbl100mesai
				
					SSKToplami = BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCG01(c)+BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCG02(c)+BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCG03(c)+ _
									BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCG04(c) +BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCG05(c)+BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KIG01(c) 
									

'msgbox BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS01(c) & "+" & BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS02(c) & "+" & BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS03(c) & "+"  & _
'									BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS04(c)  & "+" & BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS05(c) & "+" & BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AS_KIG01(c)  & "+"  & _
'									
						
					GirilmesiGirenSSK = BRDR1PBGIRIS.C_GEREKEN_SSKPRIMGUNU(c) 

'	msgbox SSKToplami & ", " & GirilmesiGirenSSK

					if SSKToplami > GirilmesiGirenSSK then
						Fark = SSKToplami - GirilmesiGirenSSK
						BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCG01(c) = BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCG01(c)-Fark
						if BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCG01(c)<0 then BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCG01(c)=0
					End if


					if SSKToplami < GirilmesiGirenSSK then
						Fark1 = GirilmesiGirenSSK - SSKToplami
						BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCG01(c) = BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCG01(c)+Fark1
						if BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCG01(c)<0 then BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCG01(c)=0
					End if
				
				End if
			End if

			if False Then 
				MSGBOX "Hata " & Err.Number & " " & Error()
				objWorkbooks(1).Close
				objExcel.Quit
				Exit Sub
				Set objExcel=Nothing
				Set objWorkbooks=Nothing
				Set objSheet1=Nothing
			End if	
		End if
		if False Then 
			objWorkbooks(1).Close
			objExcel.Quit
			Set objExcel=Nothing
			Set objWorkbooks=Nothing
			Set objSheet1=Nothing
			Exit Sub
		End if	
	Next
	
	objWorkbooks(1).Close
	objExcel.Quit

	Set objExcel=Nothing
	Set objWorkbooks=Nothing
	Set objSheet1=Nothing
	msgbox "Dosya aktarma iþlemi bitti."
End Sub
Function xCdbl(dval)
	Dim xdbl
	xdbl=""&dval
	xdbl=Trim(xdbl)
	if Trim(xdbl)="" then
		xCdbl=0
		Exit Function
	end if
	if instr(xdbl,",")>0 then
		xdbl=RePlace(xdbl,",",".")
	end if
	xCdbl=Doc.Cdbl(xdbl)
End Function

Sub Makro1()
	QStr = "SELECT * FROM LSTOK10A WHERE (evraktype='KD' OR EVRAKTYPE='K2') AND EVRAKNO IN " & _
	"(" & _
	"SELECT DISTINCT EVRAKNO FROM LSTOK10A WHERE (evraktype='KD' OR EVRAKTYPE='K2') AND SERINO IN " & _
	"(SELECT SERINO " & _
	"FROM STOK01 " & _
	"WHERE SF_MIKTAR<0 AND (KOD>='1001' AND KOD<='1001ZZZZ') " & _
	") " & _
	") " & _
	"ORDER BY EVRAKNO, trnum, EVRAKTYPE DESC"
	Set LS10A = Doc.RunSQLQuery("sdfs4sgsrwrgw", QStr)
	Set LL = Doc.Select2("sdvgew4hsh", LS10A.GetAppName, "EVRAKNO='" & STOK25E.EVRAKNO & "'", "", -1)
	Set LL2 = Doc.Select2G("sdsgsdf4f4f", LL.GetAppName, "", "", "KOD", -1)

	QStr1 = "KOD='" & LL2.KOD(1) &"'&AMBCODE='" & LL2.AMBCODE(1) & "'"
	Set S01List = Doc.Select1("sdw4gs", "STOK01", QStr1, "", -1)
	For i = 2 to LL2.GetRowCount()
		QStr1 = "KOD='" & LL2.KOD(i) &"'&AMBCODE='" & LL2.AMBCODE(i) & "'"
		Set S01List1 = Doc.Select1("sdw4gscccc", "STOK01", QStr1, "", -1)
		S01List.AppendTable(S01List1)
	Next
	For i = 1 to LL.GetRowCount()
		STOK25E.TARIH = LL.TARIH(i)
		STOK25E.AMBCODE = LL.AMBCODE
		STOK25E.TARGETAMBCODE = LL.AMBCODE

		if trim(LL.EVRAKTYPE(i))= "KD" Then
			DagitilacakMiktar = Abs(LL.SF_MIKTAR(i))
			Set K2 = Doc.Select2("sdwegsgs", LL.GetAppName, "EVRAKTYPE='K2'&EVRAKNO='" & LL.EVRAKNO(i) & "'&TRNUM='" & LL.TRNUM(i) & "'", "", -1)
			For j = 1 to S01List.GetRowCount()
				if S01List.KOD(j) = LL.KOD(i) and S01List.SF_MIKTAR(j) > 0 Then
					if S01List.SF_MIKTAR(j) >= DagitilacakMiktar Then
						DagitilanMiktar = DagitilacakMiktar
					Else
						DagitilanMiktar = S01List.SF_MIKTAR(j)
					End if
					DagitilacakMiktar = DagitilacakMiktar - DagitilanMiktar
					S01List.SF_MIKTAR(j) = S01List.SF_MIKTAR(j) - DagitilanMiktar
					satirNo = STOK25T.AddRow
					STOK25T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
					STOK25T.KOD = LL.KOD(i)
					STOK25T.LOTNUMBER = S01List.LOTNUMBER(j)
					STOK25T.SERINO = S01List.SERINO(j)
					STOK25T.SF_MIKTAR = DagitilanMiktar

					STOK25T.NEWCODE = LL.KOD(i)
					STOK25T.NEWLOTNUMBER = K2.LOTNUMBER
					STOK25T.NEWSERINO = K2.SERINO
					if DagitilacakMiktar = 0 Then Exit For
				End if
			Next
		End if
	Next
End Sub
