Sub Makro1()
	Table = "SRVPZ0"
	Call PROSEDURAKTAR(Table)
End Sub
'-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub PROSEDURAKTAR(Table)
	'klasör içinde dosya seçerek yolunu alma
	Dim objShell
	Dim strFileName
	Dim strFilePath
	Dim objFile

	Set objShell = CreateObject("Shell.Application")
	Set objFile = objShell.BrowseForFolder(0, "Choose a file:", &H4000) 'erroring here when selected file
	strFileName = objFile.Title
	strFilePath = objFile.self.Path

	dosyayolu =  strFilePath

	Set objFileName = Nothing
	Set objFilePath = Nothing
	Set objShell = Nothing
	'-------------------------------------------------------------------

	Set ExcelApp = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
	Set Dosya = ExcelApp.Workbooks.Open(dosyayolu)
	Set Sheet = Dosya.Sheets(1)
	
	Son_Satir = sheet.UsedRange.Rows.Count

	'Aktarmaya 2.satýrdan itibaren baþlayacak
	i=2
	
	While Trim(Sheet.Cells(i,1).Value) <> ""
		ALAN_Si_K1 = Trim(Sheet.Cells(i,1).Value) 'KOD       Açýklama : ALAN_S(Satýr)i(index)_K(kolon)1(kolonno) 
		ALAN_Si_K2 = Trim(Sheet.Cells(i,2).Value)	'AÇIKLAMA
		ALAN_Si_K34 = Trim(Sheet.Cells(i,3).Value) & 	Trim(Sheet.Cells(i,4).Value) 'NOTES	
		
		Set LISTE = Doc.Select1("fdsfsf", Table, "KOD='"&ALAN_Si_K1&"'", "", -1)
		If LISTE.GetRecCount = 0 Then
			Doc.Load_Voucher(Trim(ALAN_Si_K1))
				LISTE.ACIKLAMA	= ALAN_Si_K2
				LISTE.NOTES	= ALAN_Si_K34
			Doc.Save_Voucher
			Doc.LoadEmpty_Voucher
			i = i + 1
		End If
		i = i + 1
	Wend
	Dosya.Close
	Set Sheet = Nothing
	Set ExcelApp = Nothing
	End Sub

	Sub CHECKLIST_AKTAR()

	'klasör içinde dosya seçerek yolunu alma
	Dim objShell
	Dim strFileName
	Dim strFilePath
	Dim objFile

	Set objShell = CreateObject("Shell.Application")
	Set objFile = objShell.BrowseForFolder(0, "Choose a file:", &H4000) 'erroring here when selected file
	strFileName = objFile.Title
	strFilePath = objFile.self.Path

	dosyayolu =  strFilePath

	Set objFileName = Nothing
	Set objFilePath = Nothing
	Set objShell = Nothing
	'-------------------------------------------------------------------

	Set ExcelApp = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
	Set Dosya = ExcelApp.Workbooks.Open(dosyayolu)
	Set Sheet = Dosya.Sheets(1)
	
	Son_Satir = sheet.UsedRange.Rows.Count

	'Aktarmaya 2.satýrdan itibaren baþlayacak
	i=2
	
	While Trim(Sheet.Cells(i,1).Value) <> ""
		ALAN_Si_K1 = Trim(Sheet.Cells(i,1).Value) 'KOD       Açýklama : ALAN_S(Satýr)i(index)_K(kolon)1(kolonno) 
		ALAN_Si_K2 = Trim(Sheet.Cells(i,2).Value)	'AÇIKLAMA	
		ALAN_Si_K3 = Trim(Sheet.Cells(i,3).Value)	'ÝÞLEM
		
		Set SPL0 = Doc.Select1("fdsfsf", "SRVPL0", "KOD='"&ALAN_Si_K1&"'", "", -1)
		If SPL0.GetRecCount = 0 Then
			Doc.Load_Voucher(Trim(ALAN_Si_K1))
				SRVPL0.ACIKLAMA	= ALAN_Si_K2
				SRVPL0.ISLEM	= ALAN_Si_K3
			Doc.Save_Voucher
			Doc.LoadEmpty_Voucher
			i = i + 1
		End If
		i = i + 1
	Wend
	Dosya.Close
	Set Sheet = Nothing
	Set ExcelApp = Nothing
	End Sub

	Sub KALIBRASYON_AKTAR()
	'KALÝBRASYON CÝHAZ KARTI AKTARMA MAKROSU
	'klasör içinde dosya seçerek yolunu alma
	Dim objShell
	Dim strFileName
	Dim strFilePath
	Dim objFile

	Set objShell = CreateObject("Shell.Application")
	Set objFile = objShell.BrowseForFolder(0, "Choose a file:", &H4000) 'erroring here when selected file
	strFileName = objFile.Title
	strFilePath = objFile.self.Path

	dosyayolu =  strFilePath

	Set objFileName = Nothing
	Set objFilePath = Nothing
	Set objShell = Nothing
	'-------------------------------------------------------------------

	Set ExcelApp = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
	Set Dosya = ExcelApp.Workbooks.Open(dosyayolu)
	Set Sheet = Dosya.Sheets(1)
	'ExcelApp.Visible = True
	
	Son_Satir = sheet.UsedRange.Rows.Count

	'Aktarmaya 2.satýrdan itibaren baþlayacak
	i=2
	
	While Trim(Sheet.Cells(i,1).Value) <> ""
		ALAN_Si_K1 = Trim(Sheet.Cells(i,1).Value) 'KOD       Açýklama : ALAN_S(Satýr)i(index)_K(kolon)1(kolonno) 
		ALAN_Si_K2 = Trim(Sheet.Cells(i,2).Value)	'ADI	
		ALAN_Si_K3 = Trim(Sheet.Cells(i,3).Value)	'MARKASI
		ALAN_Si_K4 = Trim(Sheet.Cells(i,4).Value)	'MODELÝ
		ALAN_Si_K5 = Trim(Sheet.Cells(i,5).Value)	'ÖLÇÜM ARALIÐI
		ALAN_Si_K6 = Trim(Sheet.Cells(i,6).Value)	'HASSASÝYET
		ALAN_Si_K7 = Trim(Sheet.Cells(i,7).Value)	'SERÝ NO
		ALAN_Si_K8 = Trim(Sheet.Cells(i,8).Value)	'BÖLÜMÜ
		
		'msgbox ALAN_Si_K1 & "/" & ALAN_Si_K2 & "/" & ALAN_Si_K3 & "/" & ALAN_Si_K4 & "/" & ALAN_Si_K5 & "/" & ALAN_Si_K6 & "/" & ALAN_Si_K7 & "/" & ALAN_Si_K8

		Doc.Load_Voucher(Trim(ALAN_Si_K1))
			SRVKC0.AD1 	= ALAN_Si_K2
			SRVKC0.MODEL	= ALAN_Si_K3 & " - " & ALAN_Si_K4
			SRVKC0.OLCUM_ARALIGI 	= ALAN_Si_K5
			SRVKC0.OLCUM_HASSASIYETI	 	= ALAN_Si_K6
			SRVKC0.SERINO 	= ALAN_Si_K7
			SRVKC0.LOKASYON 	= ALAN_Si_K8
		Doc.Save_Voucher
		Doc.LoadEmpty_Voucher
		i = i + 1
	Wend
	Dosya.Close
	Set Sheet = Nothing
	Set ExcelApp = Nothing
	End Sub

Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	Set Excel = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
	Set Wb1 = Excel.Workbooks.Add	
	Set Sh1 = Wb1.Sheets.Add

	TabloAdi = Trim(Doc.GetDocType)
	Set LISTE = Doc.Select1("df3gf34gs", TabloAdi, "", "", -1) 
	
	For i = 1 To LISTE.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = satirNo + 1
		Sh1.Cells(satirNo,1).Value = Trim(LISTE.KOD(i))
		Sh1.Cells(satirNo,2).Value = Trim(LISTE.ACIKLAMA(i))
		
		If Len(LISTE.NOTES(i)) > 250 Then
			Sh1.Cells(satirNo,3).Value = Left(LISTE.NOTES(i), 250)
			Sh1.Cells(satirNo,4).Value = Mid(LISTE.NOTES(i), 251)
		Else
			Sh1.Cells(satirNo,3).Value = LISTE.NOTES(i)
		End If
	Next
	
	Excel.Visible = True
End Sub

Ekran Formu

Baþlangýçta Bu Makro Çalýþacak : 
Alt Ekranlar Ýçin Ekran Formu

Yeni Alt Ekra Form Kodu: 
Alt Ekran Açýlmadan Önce Çalýþtýrýlacak Makro :

SET DEFAULTFONT=default_font
SET DEFAULTFONT_TEXT=default_text_font
SET DEFAULTFONT_EDITTEXT=default_edittext_font
SET DEFAULTFONT_BUTTON=default_button_font
SET DEFAULTFONT_CHECKBOX=default_checkbox_font
SET DEFAULTFONT_GROUPBOX=default_groupbox_font
SET DEFAULTFONT_TABCAPTION=default_tabcaption_font

DEFINEFONT ID="FONT010" FACE="MS Sans Serif" POINT=8 BOLD=Y
DEFINEFONT ID="FONT011" FACE="MS Sans Serif" POINT=8

SET DEFAULTFONT_SELECTEDTABCAPTION=defaultfont_selectedtabcaption
SET DEFAULTFONT_UNSELECTEDTABCAPTION=defaultfont_unselectedtabcaption

Sub Makro1()
'	Set DY0001T2=Doc.GetTableObject("DY0001T2")
	Set GECOSST=Doc.Select1("_13123123","GECOSST","EVRAKNO='|D7LIC'","SRNUM",-1)
	DY0001T2.Empty()
	for i=1 to GECOSST.GetRecCount()
		RecNo=DY0001T2.AddRow()
		DY0001T2.SetCurrentRow(RecNo)
		DY0001T2.MODUL(RecNo)=GECOSST.KOD(i)
	next
	DY0001T2.SetCurrentRow(1)
End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : YILDIZ_2014 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : KASA-1
Hangi Tablo için : STOK60
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : GERÝ DÖNÜÞÜMLÜ KASALARI HESAPLA - ok - mozan - 04.12.2014
Kod : 
'by mozan
Public KasaKodu(100), KasaIciAdet(100), TanimliKasaTipiSayisi

Sub Makro1()
	For i = 1 to STOK60T.GetRowCount()
		If trim(STOK60T.FATURASIZMI(i)) = "E" Then
			STOK60T.SetCurrentRow(i)
			
			STOK60T.KOD(i) = ""
			STOK60T.LOTNUMBER(i) = ""
			STOK60T.SERINO(i) = ""
			STOK60T.SF_MIKTAR(i) = 0
			STOK60T.SF_SF_UNIT(i) = ""
			STOK60T.BRUTAGIRLIK(i) = ""
		End If
	Next
	STOK60T.SafePack()

	Set S60T2 = Doc.Select2("XXX_STOK60T_KASA2", "STOK60T", "FATURASIZMI='E'", "", -1)
	Set S60T = Doc.Select2G("XXX_STOK60T_KASA", "STOK60T", "FATURASIZMI<>'E'", "", "KOD", -1)
	KayitSayisi = S60T.GetRowCount

	If KayitSayisi = 0 Then Exit Sub
	
	For i = 1 To KayitSayisi
		StokKodu = S60T.KOD(i)
		IslemMiktari = S60T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		CariKodu = STOK60E.CH_KODU
		SevkKodu = STOK60E.SHIPTOCODE

		Call GetKasaInfo(StokKodu, CariKodu, SevkKodu)

		For j=0 to UBound(KasaKodu)-1
		
			S60T2.AddRow()
			satirNo = S60T2.GetRowCount()
			S60T2.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
			
			SevkMiktari = IslemMiktari
			KasaKodu_Value = KasaKodu(j)
			KasaIciAdet_Value = KasaIciAdet(j)
			
			If Trim(KasaKodu_Value) = "5010 KON-A1208" Or Trim(KasaKodu_Value) = "5010 KON-P80X120" Then
				KMiktar = KasaIciAdet_Value
			Else
				'KMiktarRef = Doc.Ceil(Doc.Div(SevkMiktari, KasaIciAdet_Value))
				KMiktarRef = KasaIciAdet_Value
			End If
			
			If KasaKodu_Value <> "" Then
				S60T2.KOD = KasaKodu_Value
				If Trim(KasaKodu_Value) = "5010 KON-A1208" Or Trim(KasaKodu_Value) = "5010 KON-P80X120" Then	
					S60T2.BRUTAGIRLIK = KMiktar
					S60T2.SF_STOK_MIKTAR=KMiktar
				Else
					S60T2.BRUTAGIRLIK = KMiktarRef
					S60T2.SF_MIKTAR = Doc.Ceil(Doc.Div(SevkMiktari, KMiktarRef))
					S60T2.SF_STOK_MIKTAR=KMiktarRef
					S60T2.SF_VRI_VR_R1 = "XXX"
				End If
				S60T2.SF_VRI_NUM1 = SevkMiktari

				S60T2.SF_SF_UNIT="ADET"

			End If
		Next
		
		S60T2.GroupBy("KOD")
		S60T2.SafePack
		S60T2.SORT("<FATURASIZMI")
		'Doc.ShowTableDynForm2 "XXX_STOK60T_KASA2", "KOD, SF_MIKTAR, FATURASIZMI, BRUTAGIRLIK, SF_VRI_NUM1, SF_VRI_NUM2, SF_VRI_VR_R1"

		Set S60T3 = Doc.Select2("d88fw3fsdf", S60T2.GetAppName, "SF_VRI_VR_R1='XXX' ", "", -1)
		If S60T3.GetRecCount > 0 Then 
			KutuSayisi = S60T3.SF_MIKTAR(1)
			KutuKodu = S60T3.KOD(1)
		End If
	
		S60T2.Empty
		Set S60T2 = Doc.Select2("XXX_STOK60T_KASA2", "STOK60T", "FATURASIZMI='E'", "", -1)
		S60T.Empty
		Set S60T = Doc.Select2G("XXX_STOK60T_KASA", "STOK60T", "FATURASIZMI<>'E'", "", "KOD", -1)
		KayitSayisi = S60T.GetRowCount

		If KayitSayisi = 0 Then Exit Sub
	
		For t = 1 To KayitSayisi
			StokKodu = S60T.KOD(t)
			IslemMiktari = S60T.SF_MIKTAR(t)
			CariKodu = STOK60E.CH_KODU
			SevkKodu = STOK60E.SHIPTOCODE

			Call GetKasaInfo(StokKodu, CariKodu, SevkKodu)

			For r=0 to UBound(KasaKodu)-1
			
				S60T2.AddRow()
				satirNo = S60T2.GetRowCount()
				S60T2.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
			
				SevkMiktari = IslemMiktari
				KasaKodu_Value = KasaKodu(r)
				KasaIciAdet_Value = KasaIciAdet(r)
				
				If Trim(KasaKodu_Value) = "5010 KON-A1208" Or Trim(KasaKodu_Value) = "5010 KON-P80X120" Then
					KMiktar = KasaIciAdet_Value
				Else
					KMiktarRef = KasaIciAdet_Value
				End If
			
				If KasaKodu_Value <> "" Then
					S60T2.KOD = KasaKodu_Value
					If Trim(KasaKodu_Value) = "5010 KON-A1208" Or Trim(KasaKodu_Value) = "5010 KON-P80X120" Then	
						S60T2.SF_MIKTAR = Doc.Ceil(Doc.Div(KutuSayisi, KMiktar))
						S60T2.SF_STOK_MIKTAR=Doc.Ceil(Doc.Div(KutuSayisi, KMiktar))
					Else
						S60T2.SF_MIKTAR = Doc.Ceil(Doc.Div(SevkMiktari, KMiktarRef))
						S60T2.SF_STOK_MIKTAR=KMiktarRef
					End If
					S60T2.SF_SF_UNIT="ADET"
				End If
			Next
		Next
		
		S60T2.GroupBy("KOD")
		S60T2.SafePack
		S60T2.SORT("<FATURASIZMI")
		'Doc.ShowTableDynForm2 "XXX_STOK60T_KASA2", "KOD, SF_MIKTAR, FATURASIZMI, BRUTAGIRLIK, SF_VRI_NUM1, SF_VRI_NUM2, SF_VRI_VR_R1"
	Next
	
	For i = 1 to S60T2.GetRowCount()
		STOK60T.AddRow()
		satirNo = STOK60T.GetRowCount()
		STOK60T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		
		STOK60T.KOD = S60T2.KOD(i)
		
		STOK60T.SF_MIKTAR = S60T2.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		STOK60T.FATURASIZMI = "E"
	Next
	STOK60T.SafePack()
End Sub
'--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub GetKasaInfo(StokKodu, CariKodu, SevkKodu)
	
	For j=0 to UBound(KasaKodu)-1
		KasaKodu(j) = ""
		KasaIciAdet(j) = 0
	Next
	
	QStr = "EVRAKNO='" & StokKodu & "'&CH_KODU='" & CariKodu & "'"
	Set S0A = Doc.Select1("sdfw4fsdf", "STOK0A", QStr, "", -1)

	If S0A.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Set S00 = Doc.Select1("f23fsdfs", "STOK00", "KOD='" & StokKodu & "'", "", -1)
		If S00.GetRowCount() > 0 Then

			KasaKodu(0) = S00.TEXT_TECH_1
			KasaIciAdet(0) = S00.B_B_TECH_1
		End if
	Else
		Set S0A1 = Doc.Select2("dfw3fsdf", S0A.GetAppName, "SHIPTOCODE='" & SevkKodu & "'", "", -1)

		If S0A1.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
						
			For j=1 to S0A1.GetRowCount()
				KasaKodu(j) = S0A1.AMBALAJKODU(j)
				KasaIciAdet(j) = S0A1.SF_PAKETICERIGI(j)
			Next
			
		Else

			KasaKodu(0) = Doc.GetCHStokKodu(S0A.AMBALAJKODU, CHKodu)
			KasaIciAdet(0) = S0A.SF_PAKETICERIGI
		End if
	End if
End Sub

Açýklama : CEVA DAN ÝNDÝRÝLEN SEVK YÜKLEME LÝSTESÝ
Kod : 
Sub Makro1
	Call EvrakNoF2
End Sub
'------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub EvrakNoF2

	TabloStr = "KTT_SEVKGNL"
	AlanAdi = "EVRAKNO"
	BrowseTable = "BRW_KTT_SEVKGNL_1"
	BrowseCaption = "CEVA dan Ýndirilen Sevk Listesi"
	
	BrowseQStr = "SELECT EVRAKNO ""Evrak No"" " & vbCrLf
	BrowseQStr = BrowseQStr & " FROM KTT_SEVKGNL " & vbCrLf
	BrowseQStr = BrowseQStr & " WHERE 1=1 " & vbCrLf
	BrowseQStr = BrowseQStr & " ORDER BY EVRAKNO DESC"

	BrowseQStr = "'" & Replace(BrowseQStr, "'", "\:q") & "'"
	BrowseFunc = "F2=DO_SQL_BRW("""&BrowseTable&""","""&AlanAdi&"=1"","&BrowseQStr&","""&BrowseCaption&""")"
	Set Tbl = Doc.GetTableObject(TabloStr)
	Set FieldDef = Tbl.GetFieldDef_E(AlanAdi)
	FieldDef.F2Fnc = BrowseFunc
	Tbl.SetFieldProperties1 AlanAdi,FieldDef
End Sub

Public S60, FabrikaKodu, STOK60E, STOK60T

Sub ASNGonder()
	Set STOK60E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK60E")
	Set STOK60T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK60T")
	SevkAdresKodu = STOK60E.SHIPTOCODE
	SevkFarkliligiMailAt=True
	mailtext="Bu otomatik gönderilen bir maildir."	& vbcrlf & vbcrlf & vbcrlf
	
	Set MTable=Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	KullaniciAdi = trim(MTable.F1)
	KullaniciSifre = trim(MTable.F2)
	Doc.RUN_APP_CANCEL

	
	if trim(KullaniciAdi) = "" or trim(KullaniciSifre) = "" Then
		k = doc.d7msgbox("Kullanýcý adý ve þifre girmelisiniz." & vbcrlf & vbcrlf & "Ýþlem tamamlanmadan kesilmiþtir", "dinamo",MB_OK)
		exit sub
	End if
	if Trim(STOK60E.XNITELIK0) = "ASN_OK" then
		k = doc.d7msgbox("Bu irsaliyenin ASN 'si daha önce gönderilmiþ" & vbcrlf & vbcrlf & "Ýþlem tamamlanmadan kesilmiþtir", "dinamo",MB_OK)
		exit sub
	end if
	SeferNumarasi = Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK60E",STOK60E.EVRAKNO,"SEFER_NO",0)
	if trim(SeferNumarasi) = "" then
		k = doc.d7msgbox("Kullanýcý tanýmlý alanlarda sefer numarasý girilmemiþ" & vbcrlf & vbcrlf & "Ýþlem tamamlanmadan kesilmiþtir", "dinamo",MB_OK)
		exit sub
	end if
	PlakaNo = STOK60E.BOX1_3
	if trim(PlakaNo) = "" then
		k = doc.d7msgbox("Plaka Numarasý girilmemiþ" & vbcrlf & vbcrlf & "Ýþlem tamamlanmadan kesilmiþtir", "dinamo", MB_OK)
		exit sub
	end if

	Call YuklemePlanlariniKontrolEt
	if trim(mailtext2)<>trim("Bu otomatik gönderilen bir maildir."	& vbcrlf & vbcrlf & vbcrlf) then	
	if trim(SevkAdresKodu)<>"CVR1B" THEN
		if trim(SevkAdresKodu)<>"AUF9A" THEN
				EH = doc.d7Msgbox( replace(mailtext2,"miþtir","ecektir") & vbcrlf & vbcrlf & vbcrlf & "Devam Etmek Ýstiyor musunuz?","dinamo",MB_YESNO)
				if EH <> 6 then 
					k = doc.d7Msgbox("Ýþlem kesilmiþtir.", "dinamo",MB_OK)
					exit sub
				end if
		end if		
	end if
	end if
	
	
	'if trim(mailtext2)<>trim("Bu otomatik gönderilen bir maildir."	& vbcrlf & vbcrlf & vbcrlf) and SevkFarkliligiMailAt=True then
		'Call SendMail2(mailtext2,SeferNo)
	'end if
			
			
	Select case trim(SevkAdresKodu)
		case "D1D4T" 				'ÝNÖNÜ KAMYON
			FabrikaKodu = "D1D4T"
		case "D1D4T-FR" 				'ÝNÖNÜ KAMYON FRP ATELYESÝ
			FabrikaKodu = "D1D4T"
		case "D1D4H" 				'ÝNÖNÜ MOTOR
			FabrikaKodu = "D1D4H"
		case "D1D4F"	 				'GÖLCÜK
			FabrikaKodu = "D1D4F"
		case "ARKOTO"	 				'GÖLCÜK
			FabrikaKodu = "D1D4F"
		case "AUF9A"					'KARTAL YP DEPO
			FabrikaKodu = "AUF9A"
		case "CVR1B"					'KARTAL YP DEPO
			FabrikaKodu = "CVR1B"
		case "D1D4Y" 				'KOCAELÝ YEDEK PARÇA
			FabrikaKodu = "D1D4Y"
		case "FWALA" 				'ROMANYA
			FabrikaKodu = "FWALA"
		case "FWALB" 				'BREZÝLYA
			FabrikaKodu = "FWALB"
		case "FWALC" 				'KUZEY AMERÝKA
			FabrikaKodu = "FWALC"
		case "FWALD" 				'RUSYA
			FabrikaKodu = "FWALD"
		case "FWALF" 				'KOZMO
			FabrikaKodu = "FWALF"
		case else

			k = Doc.d7msgbox("Sevk adresi girilmemiþ veya tanýmsýz" & vbcrlf & vbcrlf & "Ýþlem tamamlanmadan kesilmiþtir.", "dinamo",MB_OK)
			exit sub
	End Select
	if not AmbalajVarmi then
		EH = Doc.d7msgbox("Hiç konteyner gönderilmemektedir" & vbcrlf & vbcrlf & "Onaylýyor musunuz.", "dinamo"

Sub SendMail(MailTo, MailHeader, MailMessage, DosyaAdi)
	'on error resume next

	cdoSendUsingPickup = 1 
	cdoSendUsingPort = 2 
	cdoAnonymous = 0
	cdoBasic = 1
	cdoNTLM = 2 'NTLM
	
'	Kullanici = Doc.Getd7UserName
'	Kullanici = Doc.LeftJustify(Kullanici, 8)
'	mailserver = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("UDEF", "EMAILSERVER", Kullanici, "")
'	mailUser = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("UDEF", "EMAILKULLANICIADI", Kullanici, "")
'	mailPass = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("UDEF", "DATAMODEMNO", Kullanici, "")
'	mailFrom = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("UDEF", "EMAIL", Kullanici, "")

	mailserver = "192.168.1.6"
	mailUser = "freedom@yildizkalip.com"
	mailPass = "ff2552"
	SMTPPort = 587

	Set CDO = CreateObject("CDO.Message")

	CDO.From = trim(MailFrom)
	CDO.To = MailTo
	CDO.Subject = MailHeader
	CDO.TextBody = MailMessage
	
	if trim(DosyaAdi(3)) <> "" Then
		CDO.AddAttachment("file://" & DosyaAdi)
	End if
	CDO.Configuration.Fields.Item _
	("http://schemas.microsoft.com/cdo/configuration/sendusing") = cdoSendUsingPort 
	
	CDO.Configuration.Fields.Item _
	("http://schemas.microsoft.com/cdo/configuration/smtpserver") = trim(mailserver)
	
	CDO.Configuration.Fields.Item _
	("http://schemas.microsoft.com/cdo/configuration/smtpauthenticate") = cdoBasic
	
	CDO.Configuration.Fields.Item _
	("http://schemas.microsoft.com/cdo/configuration/sendusername") = trim(mailUser)
	
	CDO.Configuration.Fields.Item _
	("http://schemas.microsoft.com/cdo/configuration/sendpassword") = trim(mailPass)
	
	CDO.Configuration.Fields.Item _
	("http://schemas.microsoft.com/cdo/configuration/smtpserverport") = SMTPPort
	
	CDO.Configuration.Fields.Item _
	("http://schemas.microsoft.com/cdo/configuration/smtpusessl") = False
	
	CDO.Configuration.Fields.Item _
	("http://schemas.microsoft.com/cdo/configuration/smtpconnectiontimeout") = 60
	
	CDO.Configuration.Fields.Update
	
	On Error Resume Next
	CDO.Send
	
	If DosyaAdi(2) = "3dolcumden" Then
		If Err.Number = 0 Then
		 set CDO = Nothing
			Msgbox "E-mail Gönderildi." 
			Set KTTTBL3D2 = Doc.GetTableObject("KTT_TBL3D2")
			If DosyaAdi(1) = "Onay_2" Then KTTTBL3D2.ALAN01E(DosyaAdi(0))="E"
			If DosyaAdi(1) = "Onay_1" Then KTTTBL3D2.ALAN02E(DosyaAdi(0))="E"
		Else
		 set CDO = Nothing
		 Msgbox "E-mail Gönderilemedi. !!!"
		 	If DosyaAdi(1) = "Onay_2" Then KTTTBL3D2.ALAN01E(DosyaAdi(0))=""
			If DosyaAdi(1) = "Onay_1" Then KTTTBL3D2.ALAN02E(DosyaAdi(0))=""
		End If
	Else
		set CDO = Nothing
	End If

End Sub

Sub SabitTanimiGunncelleveKapat	
	Set STK01PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	StokKodu = Trim(STK01PDA.KOD)
	BarkodNo = Trim(STK01PDA.SERINO)
		
	YeniGuvenlikStokMiktari = STK01PDA.TANIMLIGVENLIKSTOKMIKTARI_INPUT
	YeniIadeSuresi = STK01PDA.TANIMLIIADESUREGUNU_INPUT
	
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK00", "")
	Set S00 = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK00")
	
	Doc1.Load_Voucher StokKodu
	
	S00.B_GUVENLIKSTOGU = YeniGuvenlikStokMiktari
	S00.B_B_TECH_25 = YeniIadeSuresi

	Doc1.Save_Voucher
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
	Doc.RUN_APP_OK

	Kontrol3 = True
	
	Call BARKOD_AV(BarkodNo)
End Sub
'-------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub SabitTanimlariGuncelle
	Set Par=Nothing
	Set STK01PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	StokKodu = Trim(STK01PDA.KOD)
	
	If StokKodu = "" Then 
		Msgbox "Önce Barkod Okutulmalýdýr."
		Exit Sub
	Else
		Doc.LoadSubForm_Dyn "SABITTANIMLARIGUNCELLE", SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, Par
		
		STK01PDA.TANIMLIGVENLIKSTOKMIKTARI_INPUT = STK01PDA.TANIMLIGVENLIKSTOKMIKTARI
		STK01PDA.TANIMLIIADESUREGUNU_INPUT = STK01PDA.TANIMLIIADESUREGUNU
	End If
End Sub
'-------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub IadesiGecikelerListesi
	Buton = "IadesiGecikenler"
	Call KIM_Depo_Mevcut_Query
End Sub
'-------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub KIM_DepoMevcutu
	Buton = "KIM"
	Call KIM_Depo_Mevcut_Query
End Sub
'-------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub KIM_Depo_Mevcut_Query
	SQL = "SELECT "
	SQL = SQL & " S01_KOD, "
	SQL = SQL & " AD1, "
	SQL = SQL & " AD2, "
	SQL = SQL & " LOTNO, "
	SQL = SQL & " DEPO_MEVCUTU, "
	SQL = SQL & " KTI_MEVCUTSTOGU, "
	SQL = SQL & " SF_UNIT, "
	SQL = SQL & " V1, "
	SQL = SQL & " V2, "
	SQL = SQL & " V3, "
	SQL = SQL & " S01_SERINO, "
	SQL = SQL & " S01_AMBCODE, "
	SQL = SQL & " IADESURESI_GUN, "
	SQL = SQL & " TRANSFERTARIHI, "
	SQL = SQL & " TRANSFERSAATI, "
	SQL = SQL & " KTAEVRAKNO, "
	SQL = SQL & " NOTVALUE, "
		
	SQL = SQL & " (TRANSFERTARIHI+IADESURESI_GUN) IADETARIHI, "
	
	SQL = SQL & " CASE "
	SQL = SQL & " WHEN ROUND(TO_NUMBER((TRANSFERTARIHI+IADESURESI_GUN)-((to_date(sysdate))-1)),0) <=0 THEN "
	SQL = SQL & " 'X' "
	SQL = SQL & " ELSE "
	SQL = SQL & " '' "
	SQL = SQL & " END IADEICINUYARIVERILECEKLER, "	
	
	SQL = SQL & " ROUND(TO_NUMBER((TRANSFERTARIHI+IADESURESI_GUN)-((to_date(sysdate))-1)),0)  KALANGUNSAYISI, "
	SQL = SQL & " PERSAD1, "
	SQL = SQL & " PERSAD2 "
	
	SQL = SQL & " FROM "
	SQL = SQL & " ( "
	SQL = SQL & " (SELECT KOD S01_KOD, SF_UNIT, LOTNUMBER LOTNO, SF_MIKTAR DEPO_MEVCUTU, VRI_VR_R1 V1, VRI_VR_R2 V2, VRI_VR_R3 V3, SERINO S01_SERINO, AMBCODE S01_AMBCODE, ALLOC_SYSSTATUS FROM STOK01)A "
	
	SQL = SQL & " LEFT JOIN "

	SQL = SQL & " (SELECT EVRAKTYPE, EVRAKNO,TRNUM, EVRAKNO || '' || TRNUM S10A_EVRAKNOTRNUM, KOD, AMBCODE, SERINO S10A_SERINO, VRI_VR_R1, VRI_VR_R2, VRI_VR_R3 FROM STOK10A "
	SQL = SQL & " WHERE AMBCODE IN ('KIM') "
	SQL = SQL & " AND TRIM(SERINO) IS NOT NULL "
	SQL = SQL & " )D "
	SQL = SQL & " ON (A.S01_KOD=D.KOD  "
	SQL = SQL & " AND A.S01_SERINO=D.S10A_SERINO  "
	SQL = SQL & " AND A.V1=D.VRI_VR_R1 "
	SQL = SQL & " AND A.V2=D.VRI_VR_R2 "
	SQL = SQL & " AND A.V3=D.VRI_VR_R3)	"
		
	SQL = SQL & " LEFT JOIN "
		
	SQL = SQL & " (SELECT ALAN08 NOTVALUE, ALAN07 TRANSFERSAATI, EVRAKNO KTAEVRAKNO, KTA_KEY, ALAN01 PROJE_KODU, ALAN02 BOLUM_FLAGI, ALAN03 PERSAD1, ALAN04 PERSAD2,  to_date(ALAN12, 'yyyy/mm/dd') TRANSFERTARIHI FROM KTA_STOK26T)E "
	SQL = SQL & " ON E.KTA_KEY=D.S10A_EVRAKNOTRNUM "
	
	SQL = SQL & " LEFT JOIN "

	SQL = SQL & " (SELECT KOD, AD AD1, NAME2 AD2,  B_B_TECH_25 IADESURESI_GUN FROM STOK00)B "
	SQL = SQL & " ON A.S01_KOD=B.KOD "	
	
	SQL = SQL & " LEFT JOIN "

	SQL = SQL & " (SELECT KOD, SUM(SF_MIKTAR) KTI_MEVCUTSTOGU FROM STOK01 WHERE AMBCODE IN ('KTÝ') group by KOD)C "
	SQL = SQL & " ON A.S01_KOD=C.KOD "
	
	SQL = SQL & " ) "
	
	'Doc.Clear_Cache()

	If Buton = "Makro1" Then 
		SQLY = SQL & " WHERE 1=0 "
		Set MTableS = Doc.RunSQLQuery("MAKROTABLES_KIM", SQLY)
		Buton = ""
		Exit Sub
	End If
		
	SQL = SQL & " WHERE S01_AMBCODE IN ('KIM') "
	SQL = SQL & " AND EVRAKTYPE IN ('T2') "
	SQL = SQL & " AND BOLUM_FLAGI IN ('TAKIMHANE')	"	
	SQL = SQL & " AND TRIM(S10A_SERINO) IS NOT NULL "
	SQL = SQL & " AND S01_KOD LIKE '%9050 K00 %' "
	SQL = SQL & " AND TRIM(S01_SERINO) IS NOT NULL "
	
	Set S01_KIM = Doc.RunSQLQuery("SQLResult02", SQL)
	'doc.ShowTableDynForm "SQLResult02"
	
	Set MTableS_KIM = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLES_KIM")
	
	KayitSayisi = S01_KIM.GetRecCount
	If KayitSayisi = 0 Then 
		MTableS_KIM.Empty
		Exit Sub
	End If

	MTableS_KIM.Empty
	MTableS_KIM.SetRowAID 1,1,1
	
	If Buton = "IadesiGecikenler" Then
		Set S01X_KIM = Doc.Select2("XXAABBCC", "SQLResult02", "IADEICINUYARIVERILECEKLER<>'' ", "", -1)
		MTableS_KIM.AppendTable(S01X_KIM)
	Else
		MTableS_KIM.AppendTable(S01_KIM)
	End If

	MTableS_KIM.Sort("S01_KOD")
	MTableS_KIM.Refresh
	Set S01_KIM = Nothing
	Set S01X_KIM = Nothing
End Sub
'-------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub Depo_Mevcut_Query
	SQL = "SELECT "
	SQL = SQL & " S01_KOD, "
	SQL = SQL & " AD1, "
	SQL = SQL & " AD2, "
	SQL = SQL & " LOTNO, "
	SQL = SQL & " DEPO_MEVCUTU, "
	SQL = SQL & " KTI_MEVCUTSTOGU, "
	SQL = SQL & " SF_UNIT, "
	SQL = SQL & " V1, "
	SQL = SQL & " V2, "
	SQL = SQL & " V3, "
	SQL = SQL & " S01_SERINO, "
	SQL = SQL & " S01_AMBCODE, "
	SQL = SQL & " GUVENLIKSTOKMIKTARI, "
		
	SQL = SQL & " (CASE "
	SQL = SQL & " WHEN NVL(GUVENLIKSTOKMIKTARI, 0)=0 THEN "
	SQL = SQL & " '' "
	SQL = SQL & " WHEN (NVL(KTI_MEVCUTSTOGU, 0)-NVL(GUVENLIKSTOKMIKTARI, 0))<=0 THEN "
	SQL = SQL & " 'X' "
	SQL = SQL & " ELSE "
	SQL = SQL & " '' "
	SQL = SQL & " END) GUV_STOK_UYARI_VERILCEKLER "
		
	SQL = SQL & " FROM "
	SQL = SQL & " ( "
	SQL = SQL & " (SELECT STOK03_ID S01_STOK03ID, KOD S01_KOD, SF_UNIT, LOTNUMBER LOTNO, SF_MIKTAR DEPO_MEVCUTU, VRI_VR_R1 V1, VRI_VR_R2 V2, VRI_VR_R3 V3, SERINO S01_SERINO, AMBCODE S01_AMBCODE, ALLOC_SYSSTATUS FROM STOK01)A "
	
	SQL = SQL & " LEFT JOIN "

	SQL = SQL & " (SELECT KOD, AD AD1, NAME2 AD2, B_GUVENLIKSTOGU GUVENLIKSTOKMIKTARI FROM STOK00)B "
	SQL = SQL & " ON A.S01_KOD=B.KOD "

	SQL = SQL & " LEFT JOIN "

	SQL = SQL & " (SELECT KOD, SUM(SF_MIKTAR) KTI_MEVCUTSTOGU FROM STOK01 WHERE AMBCODE IN ('KTÝ') group by KOD)C "
	SQL = SQL & " ON A.S01_KOD=C.KOD "
	
	SQL = SQL & " ) "
	
	If Buton = "Makro1" Then 
		SQLX = SQL & " WHERE 1=0 "
		Set MTableS = Doc.RunSQLQuery("MAKROTABLES", SQLX)
		Buton = ""
		Exit Sub
	End If
	
	SQL = SQL & " WHERE S01_AMBCODE IN ('KTÝ') "
	SQL = SQL & " AND TRIM(S01_SERINO) IS NOT NULL "	
	
	Set S01 = Doc.RunSQLQuery("SQLResult01", SQL)
	
	Set MTableS = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLES")
	
	KayitSayisi = S01.GetRecCount
	If KayitSayisi = 0 Then 
		MTableS.Empty
		Exit Sub
	End If

	MTableS.Empty
	MTableS.SetRowAID 1,1,1
	
	If Buton = "GuvStokAltinaDusenler" Then
		Set S01X = Doc.Select2("AABBCC", "SQLResult01", "GUV_STOK_UYARI_VERILCEKLER<>'' ", "", -1)
		MTableS.AppendTable(S01X)
	Else
		MTableS.AppendTable(S01)
	End If

	MTableS.Sort("S01_KOD")
	MTableS.Refresh
	Set S01 = Nothing
	Set S01X = Nothing
End Sub
'-------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub KTI_DepoMevcutu
	Buton = "KTÝ"
	Call Depo_Mevcut_Query
End Sub

Sub IadesiGecikenler

	Call KullanimdakiMalzemeler_Listesi(Buton)
End Sub

'-------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub KullanimdakiMalzemeler_Listesi(Buton)
	Set MTableS = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLES")
	
	MTableS.Empty

	SQL =" SELECT A.*, B.*, C.*, S10A_EVRAKNOTRNUM, S10A_EVRAKNO, S10A_EVRAKNOTRNUM, V1, V2, V3, LOTNO, "
	SQL = SQL & " E.*, S26T_MIKTAR, KTI_MEVCUTSTOGU, "

	SQL = SQL & " CASE "
	SQL = SQL & " WHEN NVL(GUVENLIKSTOKMIKTARI, 0)=0 THEN "
	SQL = SQL & " '' "
	SQL = SQL & " WHEN (NVL(KTI_MEVCUTSTOGU, 0)-NVL(GUVENLIKSTOKMIKTARI,0))<=0 THEN "
	SQL = SQL & " 'X' "
	SQL = SQL & " ELSE "
	SQL = SQL & " '' "
	SQL = SQL & " END GUV_STOK_UYARI_VERILCEKLER, "
	
	SQL = SQL & " CASE "
	SQL = SQL & " WHEN S10A_AMBCODE<>'KTÝ' THEN "
	SQL = SQL & " TO_DATE((TO_CHAR(to_date(TRANSFERTARIHI,'yyyy-mm-dd')+IADESURESI))) "
	SQL = SQL & " END IADETARIHI, "

	SQL = SQL & " CASE "
	SQL = SQL & " WHEN S10A_AMBCODE<>'KTÝ' THEN "
	SQL = SQL & " TO_CHAR(ROUND(TO_NUMBER((to_date(TRANSFERTARIHI,'yyyy-mm-dd')+IADESURESI)-((to_date(sysdate))-1)),0)) "
	SQL = SQL & " ELSE "
	SQL = SQL & " '' "
	SQL = SQL & " END KALANGUNSAYISI, "

	SQL = SQL & " CASE "
	SQL = SQL & " WHEN (S10A_AMBCODE<>'KTÝ' AND "
	SQL = SQL & " ROUND(TO_NUMBER((to_date(TRANSFERTARIHI,'yyyy-mm-dd')+IADESURESI)-((to_date(sysdate))-1)),0)<=0) THEN "
	SQL = SQL & " 'X' "
	SQL = SQL & " ELSE "
	SQL = SQL & " '' "
	SQL = SQL & " END IADEICINUYARIVERILECEKLER "

	SQL = SQL & " FROM "
	SQL = SQL & " ( "
	SQL = SQL & " (SELECT SERINO, "
	'SQL = SQL & "    SMAX(TRNUM), "
	SQL = SQL & "    EVRAKTYPE, "
	SQL = SQL & "    KOD, "
	'SQL = SQL & "    EVRAKNO S10A_EVRAKNO, "
	SQL = SQL & "    AMBCODE S10A_AMBCODE, "
	SQL = SQL & "    MAX(TARIH) TRANSFERTARIHI"
	SQL = SQL & " FROM STOK10A "
	
	SQL = SQL & " GROUP BY SERINO, "
	SQL = SQL & "    EVRAKTYPE, "
	SQL = SQL & "    KOD, "
	'SQL = SQL & "    EVRAKNO, "
	SQL = SQL & "    AMBCODE "
	SQL = SQL & " )A "
	
	SQL = SQL & " LEFT JOIN "
	SQL = SQL & "  (SELECT LOTNUMBER LOTNO, VRI_VR_R1 V1, VRI_VR_R2 V2, VRI_VR_R3 V3, KOD, SERINO, AMBCODE S01_AMBCODE,ALLOC_SYSSTATUS FROM STOK01 "
	SQL = SQL & "  )B "
	SQL = SQL & " ON (A.SERINO=B.SERINO AND A.S10A_AMBCODE=B.S01_AMBCODE) "
	
	SQL = SQL & " LEFT JOIN "
	SQL = SQL & " (SELECT AD AD1, NAME2 AD2, B_B_TECH_25 IADESURESI, KOD, B_GUVENLIKSTOGU GUVENLIKSTOKMIKTARI FROM STOK00)C "
	SQL = SQL & " ON A.KOD=C.KOD "
	
	SQL = SQL & " LEFT JOIN "
	SQL = SQL & " (SELECT TARIH, SERINO, AMBCODE, EVRAKTYPE, EVRAKNO || '' || TRNUM S10A_EVRAKNOTRNUM, EVRAKNO S10A_EVRAKNO  FROM STOK10A)D "
	SQL = SQL & " ON (A.TRANSFERTARIHI=D.TARIH AND A.SERINO=D.SERINO AND A.S10A_AMBCODE=D.AMBCODE AND A.EVRAKTYPE=D.EVRAKTYPE) "
	
	SQL = SQL & " LEFT JOIN "
	SQL = SQL & " (SELECT SF_MIKTAR S26T_MIKTAR, EVRAKNO || '' || TRNUM S26T_EVRAKNOTRNUM FROM STOK26T)F "
	SQL = SQL & " ON F.S26T_EVRAKNOTRNUM=D.S10A_EVRAKNOTRNUM "	
	
	SQL = SQL & " LEFT JOIN "
	SQL = SQL & " (SELECT KTA_KEY, ALAN01 PROJE_KODU, ALAN02 BOLUM_FLAGI, ALAN03 PERSAD1, ALAN04 PERSAD2 FROM KTA_STOK26T)E "
	SQL = SQL & " ON E.KTA_KEY=D.S10A_EVRAKNOTRNUM "
	
	SQL = SQL & " LEFT JOIN "
	SQL = SQL & " (SELECT SF_MIKTAR KTI_MEVCUTSTOGU, KOD, AMBCODE FROM STOK01 "
	SQL = SQL & " WHERE AMBCODE='KTÝ')G "
	SQL = SQL & " ON A.KOD=G.KOD	"
	
	SQL = SQL & " ) " 
	SQL = SQL & " WHERE 1= 1 "

	If Buton = "Tumu" Or Buton = "IadeGecikenler" Or Buton = "GuvStokAltinaDusenler"  Then SQL = SQL & " AND S10A_AMBCODE IN ('KTÝ','KIM') "	
	If Buton = "KTÝ" Then SQL = SQL & " AND S10A_AMBCODE IN ('KTÝ') " 
	If Buton = "KIM" Then SQL = SQL & " AND S10A_AMBCODE IN ('KIM')" 
	
	SQL = SQL & "AND B.KOD LIKE '9050 K00%' " 
	SQL = SQL & "AND A.EVRAKTYPE = 'T2'"
	SQL = SQL & "AND BOLUM_FLAGI = 'TAKIMHANE'"
	
	'SQL = SQL & "AND TRIM(A.SERINO) IS NOT NULL "
	'SQL = SQL & "AND TRIM(B.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS) IS NULL "
	
	SQL = SQL & " ORDER BY KALANGUNSAYISI, S10A_AMBCODE " & vbCrLf
		
	If Buton = "IadeGecikenler" Then 
		Set SS01X = Doc.RunSQLQuery("A11", SQL)
		Set SS01 = Doc.Select2("dsadad", "A22", "IADEICINUYARIVERILECEKLER<>'' ", "", -1)
	ElseIf Buton = "GuvStokAltinaDusenler" Then 
		Set SS01X = Doc.RunSQLQuery("A33", SQL)
		Set SS01 = Doc.Select2("dsadad", "A44", "GUV_STOK_UYARI_VERILCEKLER<>'' ", "", -1)
	Else
		Set SS01 = Doc.RunSQLQuery("A55", SQL)
	End If
	
	'doc.ShowTableDynForm "EEE234SFSDET"
	'Call SS01.PrintOut("", "E", false, "")  

	KayitSayisi = SS01.GetRecCount
	
	If KayitSayisi > 0 Then
		For i = 1 to KayitSayisi
			satirNo = MTableS.AddRow
			MTableS.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
			
			MTableS.KOD = SS01.KOD(i)
			MTableS.SERINO = SS01.SERINO(i)
			MTableS.AMBCODE = SS01.S10A_AMBCODE(i)
			MTableS.TRANSFERTARIHI = SS01.TRANSFERTARIHI(i)
			MTableS.IADESURESI_GUN = Trim(SS01.IADESURESI(i))
			MTableS.IADEEDILMESIGEREKENTARIH = SS01.IADETARIHI(i)
			MTableS.IADEICINKALANGUN = Trim(SS01.KALANGUNSAYISI(i))
			MTableS.KTI_MEVCUT_STOK_DURUMU = SS01.KTI_MEVCUTSTOGU(i)
			
			MTableS.GUVENLIKSTOKMIKTARI = SS01.GUVENLIKSTOKMIKTARI(i)
			MTableS.GUVENLIKSTOGUUYARISI = Trim(SS01.GUV_STOK_UYARI_VERILCEKLER(i))
			MTableS.IADEGECIKENLERUYARISI = Trim(SS01.IADEICINUYARIVERILECEKLER(i))
			MTableS.S26TEVRAKNO = Trim(SS01.S10A_EVRAKNO(i))		
			MTableS.PROJEKODU = Trim(SS01.PROJE_KODU(i))
			MTableS.BOLUMAYRAC = Trim(SS01.BOLUM_FLAGI(i))
			MTableS.PERSAD1 = Trim(SS01.PERSAD1(i))
			MTableS.PERSAD2 = Trim(SS01.PERSAD2(i))	
			MTableS.TRANSMIKTAR = Trim(SS01.S26T_MIKTAR(i))	
			MTableS.AD1 = Trim(SS01.AD1(i))	
			MTableS.AD2 = Trim(SS01.AD2(i))	
			MTableS.LOTNUMBER = Trim(SS01.LOTNO(i))				
			MTableS.VRI_VR_R1 = Trim(SS01.V1(i))				
			MTableS.VRI_VR_R2 = Trim(SS01.V2(i))	
			MTableS.VRI_VR_R3 = Trim(SS01.V3(i))					
		Next
		'Call MTableS.PrintOut("", "E", false, "")  		
	End If
	Set SS01 = Nothing
	Set SS01X = Nothing
End Sub
'-------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub TransferTablosunaEkle
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET")
	Set MTableP = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLEP")
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set STK01PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	
	AlanDepo = Trim(MTable.ALANDEPO)
	VerenDepo = Trim(MTable.VERENDEPO)
	
	AlanVeren = ""
	AlanVeren2 = ""
	
	AlanVeren = MTableP.ALANVERENSECIMI
	If AlanVeren = "1" Then AlanVeren = "V"
	If AlanVeren = "2" Then AlanVeren = "A"
	Personel1 = AlanVeren & "-" & Trim(MTableP.PERSONEL1)
	
	AlanVeren2 = MTableP.ALANVERENSECIMI2
	If AlanVeren2 = "1" Then AlanVeren2 = "V"
	If AlanVeren2 = "2" Then AlanVeren2 = "A"
	Personel2 = AlanVeren2 & "-" & MTableP.PERSONEL2
	
	If VerenDepo = "KTÝ" Then
		KontrolEdilecekPersonel = Personel2
		MalzemeKodu = STK01PDA.KOD
		Nereden = "PersonelKontrol"
		Set Tbl_PersKontrol = Nothing
		Call Personel_Daha_Once_Almismi_Kontrolu(MalzemeKodu, KontrolEdilecekPersonel)
		If Tbl_PersKontrol.GetRecCount > 0 Then
			k = Doc.d7MsgBox("Bu Personel, Ayný Malzemeden Daha Önce Almýþ." & vbcrlf & vbcrlf & "Ýþlem Kesilsin Mi?", "Freedom Uyarý", MB_YESNO)
			If k=6 Then Exit Sub
		End If
	End If
	
	MalzemeKodu = Trim(STK01PDA.KOD)
	LotNo = Trim(STK01PDA.LOTNUMBER)

	KalanMiktar = STK01PDA.KALANMIKTAR
	TransferMiktari = STK01PDA.TRANSFERMIKTARI
	
	KodLotTransferi = False

	If KalanMiktar>0 Then
		SeriNo = Doc.Sayac0danAl("SERINO")
		OncekiSeriNo = Trim(STK01PDA.SERINO) 
	
		Set D7LB_Eski = Doc.Select1("dee3ff44ghg7y66", "D7KIDSLB", "KOD='" & OncekiSeriNo & "'", "", -1)
		If D7LB_Eski.GetRecCount>0 Then IlkSeriNo = Trim(D7LB_Eski.ILKETIKET(1))
		If IlkSeriNo = "" Then IlkSeriNo = OncekiSeriNo
		
		KodLotTransferi = True
	Else
		SeriNo = Trim(STK01PDA.SERINO) 
		OncekiSeriNo = Trim(STK01PDA.SERINO) 
		Set D7LB_Eski = Doc.Select1("dee3ff44ghg7y66", "D7KIDSLB", "KOD='" & OncekiSeriNo & "'", "", -1)
		If D7LB_Eski.GetRecCount>0 Then 
			IlkSeriNo = Trim(D7LB_Eski.ILKETIKET(1))
		Else
			IlkSeriNo = ""
		End If
		
		KodLotTransferi = False
	End If
	
	ProjeKodu = Trim(MTableP.PROJEKODU)
	NotDegeri = Trim(STK01PDA.NOTE)
	
	Varyant1 = Trim(STK01PDA.VRI_VR_R1)
	Varyant2 = Trim(STK01PDA.VRI_VR_R2)
	Varyant3 = Trim(STK01PDA.VRI_VR_R3)	
	TransferSaati = Doc.Saat(5)
		
	If AlanDepo = "" Then
		Mesaj = Mesaj & " (Alan Depo) "
	End If

	If VerenDepo = "" Then
		Mesaj = Mesaj & " (Veren Depo) "
	End If	

	If Personel1 = "" Then
		Mesaj = Mesaj & " (Personel 1) "
	End If	

	If Personel2 = "" Then
		Mesaj = Mesaj & " (Personel 2) "
	End If		
	
	If MalzemeKodu = "" Then
		Mesaj = Mesaj & " (Malzeme Kodu) "
	End If		

	If SeriNo = "" Then
		Mesaj = Mesaj & " (Seri No) "
	End If	
	
	If TransferMiktari = 0 Then
		Mesaj = Mesaj & " (Transfer Miktarý) "
	End If		
	
	If Trim(Mesaj) <> "" Then
		Msgbox Mesaj & " Bilgileri Eksik."
		Exit Sub
	End If
	
	satirNo = MTableT.AddRow
	MTableT.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
			
	MTableT.KOD = MalzemeKodu
	MTableT.SetFieldValueNV "SERINO", -1, SeriNo
	
	MTableT.SF_MIKTAR = TransferMiktari
	MTableT.TARGETAMBCODE_TR = AlanDepo
	MTableT.AMBCODE_TR = VerenDepo
	MTableT.SF_VRI_VR_R1 = Varyant1
	MTableT.SF_VRI_VR_R2 = Varyant2
	MTableT.SF_VRI_VR_R3 = Varyant3
	MTableT.MT_PROJEKODU = ProjeKodu
	
	MTableT.VERENEVRAKNO = STK01PDA.S26TEVRAKNO
	MTableT.VERENEVRAKTRNUM = STK01PDA.S26TTRNUM

	MTableT.TRANSFERSAATI = TransferSaati
	MTableT.NOTE = NotDegeri
	MTableT.ILKSERINO = IlkSeriNo
	MTableT.ONCEKISERINO = OncekiSeriNo
	MTableT.TRANSFERTARIHI = Doc.StrToNorDate(Doc.Bugun)
	
	MTableT.PERSKODU1 = Personel1
	MTableT.PERSKODU2 = Personel2
	
	If KodLotTransferi Then
		MTableT.KODLOTTRANSFERI = "E"
	Else
		MTableT.KODLOTTRANSFERI = "H"
	End If
	
	MTableT.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS = STK01PDA.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS 
	MTableT.ALLOC_NONMOVABL = STK01PDA.ALLOC_NONMOVABL
	MTableT.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE = STK01PDA.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE
	MTableT.ALLOC_EVRAKNO = STK01PDA.ALLOC_EVRAKNO
	MTableT.ALLOC_SIPTYPE = STK01PDA.ALLOC_SIPTYPE
	MTableT.ALLOC_SIPNO = STK01PDA.ALLOC_SIPNO
	MTableT.ALLOC_SIPARTNO = STK01PDA.ALLOC_SIPARTNO
	MTableT.ALLOC_MPSEVNO = STK01PDA.ALLOC_MPSEVNO
	MTableT.ALLOC_MPSARTNO = STK01PDA.ALLOC_MPSARTNO
	
	MTableT.SafePack
	STK01PDA.Empty
	MTable.Empty
End Sub

Sub Personel_Daha_Once_Almismi_Kontrolu(MalzemeKodu, KontrolEdilecekPersonel)
	SQL = "SELECT "
	SQL = SQL & " S01_KOD, "
	SQL = SQL & " AD1, "
	SQL = SQL & " AD2, "
	SQL = SQL & " LOTNO, "
	SQL = SQL & " DEPO_MEVCUTU, "
	SQL = SQL & " KTI_MEVCUTSTOGU, "
	SQL = SQL & " SF_UNIT, "
	SQL = SQL & " V1, "
	SQL = SQL & " V2, "
	SQL = SQL & " V3, "
	SQL = SQL & " S01_SERINO, "
	SQL = SQL & " S01_AMBCODE, "
	SQL = SQL & " IADESURESI_GUN, "
	SQL = SQL & " TRANSFERTARIHI, "
	SQL = SQL & " TRANSFERSAATI, "
	SQL = SQL & " KTAEVRAKNO, "
	SQL = SQL & " NOTVALUE, "
		
	SQL = SQL & " (TRANSFERTARIHI+IADESURESI_GUN) IADETARIHI, "
	
	SQL = SQL & " CASE "
	SQL = SQL & " WHEN ROUND(TO_NUMBER((TRANSFERTARIHI+IADESURESI_GUN)-((to_date(sysdate))-1)),0) <=0 THEN "
	SQL = SQL & " 'X' "
	SQL = SQL & " ELSE "
	SQL = SQL & " '' "
	SQL = SQL & " END IADEICINUYARIVERILECEKLER, "	
	
	SQL = SQL & " ROUND(TO_NUMBER((TRANSFERTARIHI+IADESURESI_GUN)-((to_date(sysdate))-1)),0)  KALANGUNSAYISI, "
	SQL = SQL & " PERSAD1, "
	SQL = SQL & " PERSAD2 "
	
	SQL = SQL & " FROM "
	SQL = SQL & " ( "
	SQL = SQL & " (SELECT KOD S01_KOD, SF_UNIT, LOTNUMBER LOTNO, SF_MIKTAR DEPO_MEVCUTU, VRI_VR_R1 V1, VRI_VR_R2 V2, VRI_VR_R3 V3, SERINO S01_SERINO, AMBCODE S01_AMBCODE, ALLOC_SYSSTATUS FROM STOK01)A "
	
	SQL = SQL & " LEFT JOIN "

	SQL = SQL & " (SELECT EVRAKTYPE, EVRAKNO,TRNUM, EVRAKNO || '' || TRNUM S10A_EVRAKNOTRNUM, KOD, AMBCODE, SERINO S10A_SERINO, VRI_VR_R1, VRI_VR_R2, VRI_VR_R3 FROM STOK10A "
	SQL = SQL & " WHERE AMBCODE IN ('KIM') "
	SQL = SQL & " AND TRIM(SERINO) IS NOT NULL "
	SQL = SQL & " )D "
	SQL = SQL & " ON (A.S01_KOD=D.KOD  "
	SQL = SQL & " AND A.S01_SERINO=D.S10A_SERINO  "
	SQL = SQL & " AND A.V1=D.VRI_VR_R1 "
	SQL = SQL & " AND A.V2=D.VRI_VR_R2 "
	SQL = SQL & " AND A.V3=D.VRI_VR_R3)	"
		
	SQL = SQL & " LEFT JOIN "
		
	SQL = SQL & " (SELECT ALAN08 NOTVALUE, ALAN07 TRANSFERSAATI, EVRAKNO KTAEVRAKNO, KTA_KEY, ALAN01 PROJE_KODU, ALAN02 BOLUM_FLAGI, ALAN03 PERSAD1, ALAN04 PERSAD2,  to_date(ALAN12, 'yyyy/mm/dd') TRANSFERTARIHI FROM KTA_STOK26T)E "
	SQL = SQL & " ON E.KTA_KEY=D.S10A_EVRAKNOTRNUM "
	
	SQL = SQL & " 

Ekran Formu

Sub Makro1
	Set S26E = Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("STOK26E=STOK26E(NOFILE)","IO")
	S26E.Setd7FieldEvent "EVRAKNO", "AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "EvrakYukle"


	Set S26T = Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("STOK26T=STOK26T(NOFILE)","IO")
	Set ATC = Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("ATCDOC=ATCDOC(NOFILE)","IO")
End Sub

Sub EvrakYukle
	msgbox "111"
	Set S26E2 = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK26E")
	Msgbox S26E2.EVRAKNO
End Sub

Public Kontrol2, SS01, S001, SQL, Buton, QstrF2, BarkodNo, Kontrol3
'-------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub Depo_Mevcut_Query
	SQL = "SELECT "
	
	SQL = SQL & " S01_KOD, "
	SQL = SQL & " AD1, "
	SQL = SQL & " AD2, "
	SQL = SQL & " LOTNO, "
	SQL = SQL & " DEPO_MEVCUTU, "
	SQL = SQL & " KTI_MEVCUTSTOGU, "
	SQL = SQL & " SF_UNIT, "
	SQL = SQL & " V1, "
	SQL = SQL & " V2, "
	SQL = SQL & " V3, "
	SQL = SQL & " S01_SERINO, "
	SQL = SQL & " S01_AMBCODE, "
	SQL = SQL & " GUVENLIKSTOKMIKTARI, "

	SQL = SQL & " (CASE "
	SQL = SQL & " WHEN NVL(GUVENLIKSTOKMIKTARI, 0)=0 THEN "
	SQL = SQL & " '' "
	SQL = SQL & " WHEN (NVL(KTI_MEVCUTSTOGU, 0)-NVL(GUVENLIKSTOKMIKTARI, 0))<=0 THEN "
	SQL = SQL & " 'X' "
	SQL = SQL & " ELSE "
	SQL = SQL & " '' "
	SQL = SQL & " END) GUV_STOK_UYARI_VERILCEKLER "
		
	SQL = SQL & " FROM "
	SQL = SQL & " ( "
	SQL = SQL & " (SELECT KOD S01_KOD, SF_UNIT, LOTNUMBER LOTNO, SF_MIKTAR DEPO_MEVCUTU, VRI_VR_R1 V1, VRI_VR_R2 V2, VRI_VR_R3 V3, SERINO S01_SERINO, AMBCODE S01_AMBCODE, ALLOC_SYSSTATUS FROM STOK01)A "
	
	If Buton = "KIM" Then 
		SQL = SQL & " LEFT JOIN "

		SQL = SQL & " (SELECT EVRAKTYPE, EVRAKNO,TRNUM, EVRAKNO || '' || TRNUM S10A_EVRAKNOTRNUM, KOD, AMBCODE, SERINO S10A_SERINO, VRI_VR_R1, VRI_VR_R2, VRI_VR_R3 FROM STOK10A "
		SQL = SQL & " WHERE AMBCODE IN ('KIM') "
		SQL = SQL & " AND TRIM(SERINO) IS NOT NULL "
		SQL = SQL & " )D "
		SQL = SQL & " ON (A.S01_KOD=D.KOD  "
		SQL = SQL & " AND A.S01_SERINO=D.S10A_SERINO  "
		SQL = SQL & " AND A.V1=D.VRI_VR_R1 "
		SQL = SQL & " AND A.V2=D.VRI_VR_R2 "
		SQL = SQL & " AND A.V3=D.VRI_VR_R3)	"
		
		SQL = SQL & " LEFT JOIN "
		
		SQL = SQL & " (SELECT KTA_KEY, ALAN01 PROJE_KODU, ALAN02 BOLUM_FLAGI, ALAN03 PERSAD1, ALAN04 PERSAD2 FROM KTA_STOK26T)E "
		SQL = SQL & " ON E.KTA_KEY=D.S10A_EVRAKNOTRNUM 	"
	End If
	
	SQL = SQL & " LEFT JOIN "

	SQL = SQL & " (SELECT KOD, AD AD1, NAME2 AD2, B_GUVENLIKSTOGU GUVENLIKSTOKMIKTARI FROM STOK00)B "
	SQL = SQL & " ON A.S01_KOD=B.KOD "

	SQL = SQL & " LEFT JOIN "

	SQL = SQL & " (SELECT KOD, SUM(SF_MIKTAR) KTI_MEVCUTSTOGU FROM STOK01 WHERE AMBCODE IN ('KTÝ') group by KOD)C "
	SQL = SQL & " ON A.S01_KOD=C.KOD "
	
	SQL = SQL & " ) "
	
	If Buton = "Makro1" Then 
		SQLX = SQL & " WHERE 1=0 "
		Set MTableS = Doc.RunSQLQuery("MAKROTABLES", SQLX)
		Buton = ""
		Exit Sub
	End If
	
 	'If Buton = "Tumu" Or Buton = "GuvStokAltinaDusenler" Then SQL = SQL & " WHERE S01_AMBCODE IN ('KTÝ', 'KIM') "
	If Buton = "KTÝ" Or Buton = "GuvStokAltinaDusenler" Then SQL = SQL & " WHERE S01_AMBCODE IN ('KTÝ') "

	If Buton = "KIM" Then 
		SQL = SQL & " WHERE S01_AMBCODE IN ('KIM') "
		SQL = SQL & " AND EVRAKTYPE IN ('T2') "
		SQL = SQL & " AND BOLUM_FLAGI IN ('TAKIMHANE')	"	
		SQL = SQL & " AND TRIM(S10A_SERINO) IS NOT NULL "
	End If
		
	SQL = SQL & " AND S01_KOD LIKE '%9050 K00 %' "
	SQL = SQL & " AND TRIM(S01_SERINO) IS NOT NULL "
	
	'SQL = SQL & " AND S01_KOD NOT LIKE '%ZZ%' AND S01_KOD NOT LIKE '%Y%' "

		
	Set S01 = Doc.RunSQLQuery("SQLResult01", SQL)
	
	Set MTableS = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLES")
	
	KayitSayisi = S01.GetRecCount
	If KayitSayisi = 0 Then 
		MTableS.Empty
		Exit Sub
	End If

	MTableS.Empty
	MTableS.SetRowAID 1,1,1
	
	If Buton = "GuvStokAltinaDusenler" Then
		Set S01X = Doc.Select2("AABBCC", "SQLResult01", "GUV_STOK_UYARI_VERILCEKLER<>'' ", "", -1)
		MTableS.AppendTable(S01X)
	Else
		MTableS.AppendTable(S01)
	End If

	MTableS.Sort("S01_KOD")
	MTableS.Refresh
	Set S01 = Nothing
	Set S01X = Nothing
End Sub
'---------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub Tumu_DepoMevcutu
	Buton = "Tumu"
	Call Depo_Mevcut_Query
End Sub
'-------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub KIM_DepoMevcutu
	Buton = "KIM"
	Call Depo_Mevcut_Query
End Sub
'-------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub KTI_DepoMevcutu
	Buton = "KTÝ"
	Call Depo_Mevcut_Query
End Sub

Sub Makro1
	Set STOK01_PDA=Doc.CreateTableObject("STOK01_PDA=STOK01(NOFILE)","IO")

	Set MTableP=Doc.CreateTableObject("MAKROTABLEP=STOK01","IO")

	MTable.Addfield2 "ALANDEPO", "CHAR", 10, 0, "L", "", ""
	
	SQL=""
	Buton = "Makro1"
	Call Depo_Mevcut_Query

	'MTableS.Addfield2 "IADEEDILMESIGEREKENTARIH","DATE",10,0,"C","",""
	'MTableS.Addfield2 "IADEICINKALANGUN","CHAR",10,0,"C","",""
	'MTableS.Addfield2 "KTI_MEVCUT_STOK_DURUMU","CHAR",8,2,"C","",""
	'MTableS.Addfield2 "GUVENLIKSTOKMIKTARI","CHAR",10,0,"C","",""
	'MTableS.Addfield2 "GUVENLIKSTOGUUYARISI","CHAR",10,0,"C","",""
	'MTableS.Addfield2 "IADEGECIKENLERUYARISI","CHAR",10,0,"C","",""	
	'MTableS.Addfield2 "S26TEVRAKNO","CHAR",10,0,"C","",""

	'MTableS.Addfield2 "PERSAD1","CHAR",50,0,"C","",""

Sub IadesiGecikenler
	Buton = "IadeGecikenler"
	Call KullanimdakiMalzemeler_Listesi(Buton)
End Sub
'-------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub GuvenlikStoguAltindakiler
	Buton = "GuvStokAltinaDusenler"
	Call Depo_Mevcut_Query
End Sub
'-------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub KullanimdakiMalzemeler_Listesi(Buton)
	Set MTableS = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLES")
	
	MTableS.Empty

	SQL =" SELECT A.*, B.*, C.*, S10A_EVRAKNOTRNUM, S10A_EVRAKNO, S10A_EVRAKNOTRNUM, V1, V2, V3, LOTNO, "
	SQL = SQL & " E.*, S26T_MIKTAR, KTI_MEVCUTSTOGU, "

	SQL = SQL & " CASE "
	SQL = SQL & " WHEN NVL(GUVENLIKSTOKMIKTARI, 0)=0 THEN "
	SQL = SQL & " '' "
	SQL = SQL & " WHEN (NVL(KTI_MEVCUTSTOGU, 0)-NVL(GUVENLIKSTOKMIKTARI,0))<=0 THEN "
	SQL = SQL & " 'X' "
	SQL = SQL & " ELSE "
	SQL = SQL & " '' "
	SQL = SQL & " END GUV_STOK_UYARI_VERILCEKLER, "
	
	SQL = SQL & " CASE "
	SQL = SQL & " WHEN S10A_AMBCODE<>'KTÝ' THEN "
	SQL = SQL & " TO_DATE((TO_CHAR(to_date(TRANSFERTARIHI,'yyyy-mm-dd')+IADESURESI))) "
	SQL = SQL & " END IADETARIHI, "

	SQL = SQL & " CASE "
	SQL = SQL & " WHEN S10A_AMBCODE<>'KTÝ' THEN "
	SQL = SQL & " TO_CHAR(ROUND(TO_NUMBER((to_date(TRANSFERTARIHI,'yyyy-mm-dd')+IADESURESI)-((to_date(sysdate))-1)),0)) "
	SQL = SQL & " ELSE "
	SQL = SQL & " '' "
	SQL = SQL & " END KALANGUNSAYISI, "

	SQL = SQL & " CASE "
	SQL = SQL & " WHEN (S10A_AMBCODE<>'KTÝ' AND "
	SQL = SQL & " ROUND(TO_NUMBER((to_date(TRANSFERTARIHI,'yyyy-mm-dd')+IADESURESI)-((to_date(sysdate))-1)),0)<=0) THEN "
	SQL = SQL & " 'X' "
	SQL = SQL & " ELSE "
	SQL = SQL & " '' "
	SQL = SQL & " END IADEICINUYARIVERILECEKLER "

	SQL = SQL & " FROM "
	SQL = SQL & " ( "
	SQL = SQL & " (SELECT SERINO, "
	'SQL = SQL & "    SMAX(TRNUM), "
	SQL = SQL & "    EVRAKTYPE, "
	SQL = SQL & "    KOD, "
	'SQL = SQL & "    EVRAKNO S10A_EVRAKNO, "
	SQL = SQL & "    AMBCODE S10A_AMBCODE, "
	SQL = SQL & "    MAX(TARIH) TRANSFERTARIHI"
	SQL = SQL & " FROM STOK10A "
	
	SQL = SQL & " GROUP BY SERINO, "
	SQL = SQL & "    EVRAKTYPE, "
	SQL = SQL & "    KOD, "
	'SQL = SQL & "    EVRAKNO, "
	SQL = SQL & "    AMBCODE "
	SQL = SQL & " )A "
	
	SQL = SQL & " LEFT JOIN "
	SQL = SQL & "  (SELECT LOTNUMBER LOTNO, VRI_VR_R1 V1, VRI_VR_R2 V2, VRI_VR_R3 V3, KOD, SERINO, AMBCODE S01_AMBCODE,ALLOC_SYSSTATUS FROM STOK01 "
	SQL = SQL & "  )B "
	SQL = SQL & " ON (A.SERINO=B.SERINO AND A.S10A_AMBCODE=B.S01_AMBCODE) "
	
	SQL = SQL & " LEFT JOIN "
	SQL = SQL & " (SELECT AD AD1, NAME2 AD2, B_B_TECH_25 IADESURESI, KOD, B_GUVENLIKSTOGU GUVENLIKSTOKMIKTARI FROM STOK00)C "
	SQL = SQL & " ON A.KOD=C.KOD "
	
	SQL = SQL & " LEFT JOIN "
	SQL = SQL & " (SELECT TARIH, SERINO, AMBCODE, EVRAKTYPE, EVRAKNO || '' || TRNUM S10A_EVRAKNOTRNUM, EVRAKNO S10A_EVRAKNO  FROM STOK10A)D "
	SQL = SQL & " ON (A.TRANSFERTARIHI=D.TARIH AND A.SERINO=D.SERINO AND A.S10A_AMBCODE=D.AMBCODE AND A.EVRAKTYPE=D.EVRAKTYPE) "
	
	SQL = SQL & " LEFT JOIN "
	SQL = SQL & " (SELECT SF_MIKTAR S26T_MIKTAR, EVRAKNO || '' || TRNUM S26T_EVRAKNOTRNUM FROM STOK26T)F "
	SQL = SQL & " ON F.S26T_EVRAKNOTRNUM=D.S10A_EVRAKNOTRNUM "	
	
	SQL = SQL & " LEFT JOIN "
	SQL = SQL & " (SELECT KTA_KEY, ALAN01 PROJE_KODU, ALAN02 BOLUM_FLAGI, ALAN03 PERSAD1, ALAN04 PERSAD2 FROM KTA_STOK26T)E "
	SQL = SQL & " ON E.KTA_KEY=D.S10A_EVRAKNOTRNUM "
	
	SQL = SQL & " LEFT JOIN "
	SQL = SQL & " (SELECT SF_MIKTAR KTI_MEVCUTSTOGU, KOD, AMBCODE FROM STOK01 "
	SQL = SQL & " WHERE AMBCODE='KTÝ')G "
	SQL = SQL & " ON A.KOD=G.KOD	"
	
	SQL = SQL & " ) " 
	SQL = SQL & " WHERE 1= 1 "

	If Buton = "Tumu" Or Buton = "IadeGecikenler" Or Buton = "GuvStokAltinaDusenler"  Then SQL = SQL & " AND S10A_AMBCODE IN ('KTÝ','KIM') "	
	If Buton = "KTÝ" Then SQL = SQL & " AND S10A_AMBCODE IN ('KTÝ') " 
	If Buton = "KIM" Then SQL = SQL & " AND S10A_AMBCODE IN ('KIM')" 
	
	SQL = SQL & "AND B.KOD LIKE '9050 K00%' " 
	SQL = SQL & "AND A.EVRAKTYPE = 'T2'"
	SQL = SQL & "AND BOLUM_FLAGI = 'TAKIMHANE'"
	
	'SQL = SQL & "AND TRIM(A.SERINO) IS NOT NULL "
	'SQL = SQL & "AND TRIM(B.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS) IS NULL "
	
	SQL = SQL & " ORDER BY KALANGUNSAYISI, S10A_AMBCODE " & vbCrLf
		
	If Buton = "IadeGecikenler" Then 
		Set SS01X = Doc.RunSQLQuery("A11", SQL)
		Set SS01 = Doc.Select2("dsadad", "A22", "IADEICINUYARIVERILECEKLER<>'' ", "", -1)
	ElseIf Buton = "GuvStokAltinaDusenler" Then 
		Set SS01X = Doc.RunSQLQuery("A33", SQL)
		Set SS01 = Doc.Select2("dsadad", "A44", "GUV_STOK_UYARI_VERILCEKLER<>'' ", "", -1)
	Else
		Set SS01 = Doc.RunSQLQuery("A55", SQL)
	End If
	
	'doc.ShowTableDynForm "EEE234SFSDET"
	'Call SS01.PrintOut("", "E", false, "")  

	KayitSayisi = SS01.GetRecCount
	
	If KayitSayisi > 0 Then
		For i = 1 to KayitSayisi
			satirNo = MTableS.AddRow
			MTableS.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
			
			MTableS.KOD = SS01.KOD(i)
			MTableS.SERINO = SS01.SERINO(i)
			MTableS.AMBCODE = SS01.S10A_AMBCODE(i)
			MTableS.TRANSFERTARIHI = SS01.TRANSFERTARIHI(i)
			MTableS.IADESURESI_GUN = Trim(SS01.IADESURESI(i))
			MTableS.IADEEDILMESIGEREKENTARIH = SS01.IADETARIHI(i)
			MTableS.IADEICINKALANGUN = Trim(SS01.KALANGUNSAYISI(i))
			MTableS.KTI_MEVCUT_STOK_DURUMU = SS01.KTI_MEVCUTSTOGU(i)
			
			MTableS.GUVENLIKSTOKMIKTARI = SS01.GUVENLIKSTOKMIKTARI(i)
			MTableS.GUVENLIKSTOGUUYARISI = Trim(SS01.GUV_STOK_UYARI_VERILCEKLER(i))
			MTableS.IADEGECIKENLERUYARISI = Trim(SS01.IADEICINUYARIVERILECEKLER(i))
			MTableS.S26TEVRAKNO = Trim(SS01.S10A_EVRAKNO(i))		
			MTableS.PROJEKODU = Trim(SS01.PROJE_KODU(i))
			MTableS.BOLUMAYRAC = Trim(SS01.BOLUM_FLAGI(i))
			MTableS.PERSAD1 = Trim(SS01.PERSAD1(i))
			MTableS.PERSAD2 = Trim(SS01.PERSAD2(i))	
			MTableS.TRANSMIKTAR = Trim(SS01.S26T_MIKTAR(i))	
			MTableS.AD1 = Trim(SS01.AD1(i))	
			MTableS.AD2 = Trim(SS01.AD2(i))	
			MTableS.LOTNUMBER = Trim(SS01.LOTNO(i))				
			MTableS.VRI_VR_R1 = Trim(SS01.V1(i))				
			MTableS.VRI_VR_R2 = Trim(SS01.V2(i))	
			MTableS.VRI_VR_R3 = Trim(SS01.V3(i))					
		Next
		'Call MTableS.PrintOut("", "E", false, "")  		
	End If
	Set SS01 = Nothing
	Set SS01X = Nothing
End Sub
'-------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub TransferTablosunaEkle
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET")
	Set MTableP = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLEP")
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set STK01PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	
	MalzemeKodu = Trim(STK01PDA.KOD)
	LotNo = Trim(STK01PDA.LOTNUMBER)

	AlanDepo = Trim(MTable.ALANDEPO)
	VerenDepo = Trim(MTable.VERENDEPO)
	
	KalanMiktar = STK01PDA.KALANMIKTAR
	TransferMiktari = STK01PDA.TRANSFERMIKTARI
	
	KodLotTransferi = False

	If KalanMiktar>0 Then
		SeriNo = Doc.Sayac0danAl("SERINO")
		OncekiSeriNo = Trim(STK01PDA.SERINO) 
	
		Set D7LB_Eski = Doc.Select1("dee3ff44ghg7y66", "D7KIDSLB", "KOD='" & OncekiSeriNo & "'", "", -1)
		If D7LB_Eski.GetRecCount>0 Then IlkSeriNo = Trim(D7LB_Eski.ILKETIKET(1))
		If IlkSeriNo = "" Then IlkSeriNo = OncekiSeriNo
		
		KodLotTransferi = True
	Else
		SeriNo = Trim(STK01PDA.SERINO) 
		OncekiSeriNo = Trim(STK01PDA.SERINO) 
		Set D7LB_Eski = Doc.Select1("dee3ff44ghg7y66", "D7KIDSLB", "KO

	SET FIXEDPITCH_EDIT=1
	SET DEFAULT_FSTYLE=3
	
	DEFINEFONT ID="default_font" FACE="MS Sans Serif" POINT=8
	DEFINEFONT ID="default_text_font" FACE="MS Sans Serif" POINT=8

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : YILDIZ_2014 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : STOK00-01
Hangi Tablo için : STOK00
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Sevk Yükleme Formlarý - CEVA dan Ýndirilen
Kod : 
Sub Makro1

	Dim objShell
	Dim strFileName
	Dim strFilePath
	Dim objFile
	Set objFileName = Nothing
	Set objFilePath = Nothing
	Set objShell = Nothing
	'-------------------------------------------------------------------
	LocalPath = "E:\yildiz_kalip_dosyalar\22 - FORD YÜKLEME PLANLARI" '& "\" & FTP_Firma_Klasor_SubDir
	Set FileSys = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
	Set DataFolder = FileSys.GetFolder(LocalPath) 
	Set AltKlasorler = DataFolder.SubFolders
	
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("_GNPRG55", "")
	Set KTT_SGNL = Doc1.GetTableObject("KTT_SEVKGNL")
	Set KTT_STBL1 = Doc1.GetTableObject("KTT_SEVKTBL1")
	Guncelleme = False
	
	For Each f1 in AltKlasorler
		AltKlasorAdi = s & Trim(f1.Name)
		AltKlasorAdi2 = Replace(AltKlasorAdi, ".", "")
		
		Set Table = Doc.Select1("dadasd", "KTT_SEVKGNL", "EVRAKNO='"&AltKlasorAdi2&"' ", "", -1)
		If Table.GetRecCount = 0 Then
			If Not Guncelleme Then
				k = Doc.d7MsgBox("Güncellenecek Yeni FORD YÜKLEME FORMU Var. Güncelleme Yapýlsýn mý?", "Freedom Uyarý", MB_YESNO)
				If k<>6 Then Exit Sub
			End If
			Guncelleme = True
			Doc1.Load_Voucher AltKlasorAdi2
			
			Dosyayolu = LocalPath & "\" & AltKlasorAdi & "\CQEMA_TUM_DETAY.xls"

			Set ExcelApp = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
			Set Dosya = ExcelApp.Workbooks.Open(dosyayolu)
			Set Sheet = Dosya.Sheets(1)
			
			Son_Satir = sheet.UsedRange.Rows.Count
			i=3
			
			While Trim(Sheet.Cells(i,1).Value) <> ""
				
				seferno = Trim(Sheet.Cells(i,1).Value) 	
				sevkiyatno = Trim(Sheet.Cells(i,2).Value)	
				firmayuklemetarihsaat = Trim(Sheet.Cells(i,3).Value)
				firmaayrilistarihsaat = Trim(Sheet.Cells(i,4).Value)
				plaka = Trim(Sheet.Cells(i,5).Value)
				parcanumarasi = Trim(Sheet.Cells(i,6).Value)
				kasaiciadet = Sheet.Cells(i,7).Value
				parcaadedi = Sheet.Cells(i,8).Value
				kasatipi = Trim(Sheet.Cells(i,9).Value)
				kasaadedi = Sheet.Cells(i,10).Value
				dock = Trim(Sheet.Cells(i,11).Value)
				hacim = Trim(Sheet.Cells(i,12).Value)
				agirlik = Trim(Sheet.Cells(i,13).Value)
															
				satirNo =	KTT_STBL1.AddRow()
				KTT_STBL1.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
					
				KTT_STBL1.ALAN01 = seferno
				KTT_STBL1.ALAN02 = sevkiyatno
				KTT_STBL1.ALAN03 = firmayuklemetarihsaat
				KTT_STBL1.ALAN04 = firmaayrilistarihsaat
				KTT_STBL1.ALAN05 = plaka									
				KTT_STBL1.ALAN06 = parcanumarasi
				KTT_STBL1.ALAN07 = kasaiciadet
				KTT_STBL1.ALAN08 = parcaadedi
				KTT_STBL1.ALAN09 = kasatipi
				KTT_STBL1.ALAN10 = kasaadedi
				KTT_STBL1.ALAN11 = dock
				KTT_STBL1.ALAN12 = hacim
				KTT_STBL1.ALAN13 = agirlik
				i = i + 1
			Wend
			Dosya.Close
			Doc1.Save_Voucher
			Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
		End If
	Next	
	Set Sheet = Nothing
	Set ExcelApp = Nothing
End Sub
